function sortList(list, ST, A){ ST=ST||S; A=A||GRID0; const val=A.val;

async function qdUploadFiles(C,V,slot,files,cam){ const ref=V.ctx.key; if(!files.length) return; const msg=t=>{ if($('#qdmsg')) $('#qdmsg').textContent=t; }; let V2=null, sc=null, n=0;
  for(const f of files){ try{ n++; msg('\u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u062a\u062c\u0647\u064a\u0632 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0644\u0641 '+n+' \u0645\u0646 '+files.length+'\u2026'); let b64, mime=f.type||'';
      if(mime==='application/pdf'){ if(f.size>15*1024*1024) throw new Error('\u0627\u0644\u0645\u0644\u0641 \u0623\u0643\u0628\u0631 \u0645\u0646 15 \u0645\u064a\u063a\u0627\u0628\u0627\u064a\u062a'); b64=await fileB64(f); }
      else { let im=await shrinkImg(f,1600,0.75); if(cam&&slot.indexOf('p:')===0){ sc=sc||stampCtx(ref); msg('\u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u062a\u062d\u062f\u064a\u062f \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0648\u0642\u0639 \u0648\u062e\u062a\u0645 \u0627\u0644\u0635\u0648\u0631\u0629\u2026'); im=await stampPhoto(im,sc); } b64=im.b64; mime=im.mime; var loc=im.loc; }
      msg('\u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u0627\u0644\u0631\u0641\u0639 '+n+' \u0645\u0646 '+files.length+'\u2026'); V2=await api('qdUpload',S.tok,ref,slot,b64,mime,loc||''); loc=''; }
    catch(e){ toast(e.message); } }
  if(V2){ qdApply(V2); if($('#mdl')&&$('.qdm')) qdDraw(C,V2); toast('\u062a\u0645 \u0627\u0644\u0631\u0641\u0639'); } }

const QF_GRACE=3;
const needsQF=o=>{ const e=S.pct['\u062a\u0645 \u0627\u0644\u062a\u0646\u0641\u064a\u0630']||0; return e>0 && (S.pct[o.cur_stage]??0)>=e; };
const qfMissing=o=>needsQF(o)&&!o.qf_id;
const qfWait=o=>qfMissing(o)?Math.max(0,days(S.B.today,o.exec_date||o.cur_since||S.B.today)):0;
const engOf=o=>o.site_engineer||o.engineer||'(\u063a\u064a\u0631 \u0645\u062d\u062f\u062f)';
function qfEngTable(list){
  const m={}; list.filter(o=>needsQF(o)||o.qf_status).forEach(o=>{ const k=engOf(o); const x=m[k]||(m[k]={need:0,up:0,wait:0,ok:0,rej:0,miss:0,late:0,max:0});
    if(needsQF(o)) x.need++; if(o.qf_id) x.up++; if(o.qf_status==='\u0628\u0627\u0646\u062a\u0638\u0627\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u062c\u0648\u062f\u0629') x.wait++; if(o.qf_status==='\u0645\u0639\u062a\u0645\u062f') x.ok++; if(o.qf_status==='\u0645\u0631\u0641\u0648\u0636') x.rej++;
    if(qfMissing(o)){ x.miss++; const d=qfWait(o); if(d>QF_GRACE) x.late++; x.max=Math.max(x.max,d); } });
  const rows=Object.entries(m).sort((a,b)=>(b[1].late-a[1].late)||(b[1].miss-a[1].miss));
  if(!rows.length) return '<div class="muted">\u064a\u0638\u0647\u0631 \u0628\u0639\u062f \u0648\u0635\u0648\u0644 \u0623\u0648\u0627\u0645\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0639\u0645\u0644 \u0644\u0645\u0631\u062d\u0644\u0629 \u00ab\u062a\u0645 \u0627\u0644\u062a\u0646\u0641\u064a\u0630\u00bb.</div>';
  return `<div class="tw" style="max-height:320px"><table><thead><tr><th>\u0627\u0644\u0645\u0647\u0646\u062f\u0633</th><th>\u0645\u0646\u0641\u0651\u0630\u0629</th><th>\u0645\u0631\u0641\u0648\u0639\u0629</th><th>\u0628\u0627\u0646\u062a\u0638\u0627\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u062c\u0648\u062f\u0629</th><th>\u0645\u0639\u062a\u0645\u062f\u0629</th><th>\u0645\u0631\u0641\u0648\u0636\u0629</th><th>\u0628\u0644\u0627 \u0645\u0644\u0641</th><th>\u0645\u062a\u0623\u062e\u0631\u0629 +${QF_GRACE} \u0623\u064a\u0627\u0645</th><th>\u0623\u0642\u0635\u0649 \u062a\u0623\u062e\u064a\u0631 (\u064a\u0648\u0645)</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map(([k,x])=>`<tr class="go" data-eng="${esc(k)}"><td>${esc(k)}</td><td>${x.need}</td><td>${x.up}</td><td>${x.wait}</td><td>${x.ok}</td><td class="${x.rej?'late':''}">${x.rej}</td><td class="${x.miss?'late':''}">${x.miss}</td><td class="${x.late?'late':''}"><b>${x.late}</b></td><td>${x.max||''}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`;
}

    const read=f=>new Promise((ok,no)=>{ const r=new FileReader(); r.onload=()=>ok(String(r.result).split(',')[1]); r.onerror=no; r.readAsDataURL(f); });
    for(let i=0;i<items.length;i++){ const x=items[i]; if(x.st!=='\u062c\u0627\u0647\u0632') continue; const cell=$('#qbs'+i); cell.textContent='\u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u0627\u0644\u0631\u0641\u0639\u2026';
      try{ const b64=await read(x.f); const n=await api('uploadQuality',S.tok,x.o.key,b64,'application/pdf',{bulk:true,pending:pend,skipIfExists:!rep});
        Object.assign(x.o,n); delete x.o._skipped; derive(x.o);
        if(n._skipped){ skip++; cell.textContent='\u062a\u064f\u0631\u0643 (\u0644\u0647 \u0645\u0644\u0641 \u0633\u0627\u0628\u0642)'; } else { done++; cell.textContent='\u2705 '+n.qf_status; } }
      catch(e){ fail++; cell.textContent='\u062e\u0637\u0623: '+e.message; cell.className='late'; } }
    $('#qberr').textContent=''; shell(); toast(`\u062a\u0645 \u0631\u0641\u0639 ${done} \u0645\u0644\u0641\u0627\u064b`+(skip?` \u00b7 \u062a\u064f\u0631\u0643 ${skip}`:'')+(fail?` \u00b7 \u0641\u0634\u0644 ${fail}`:'')); $('#qbf').disabled=false; };
}

let undoBusy=false;
const afterUndo=async scope=>{ if(scope==='mt'){ S.mt=null; shell(); } else if(scope){ shell(); } else { const cur=S.view; await start(); S.view=cur; } };
async function undoLast(scope){ scope=scope||''; if(undoBusy) return; undoBusy=true; const g=$('#gsave'); if(g) g.textContent='\u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u0627\u0644\u062a\u0631\u0627\u062c\u0639\u2026';
  try{ const st=await api('listSteps',S.tok,scope); if(!st.length){ toast('\u0644\u0627 \u062a\u0648\u062c\u062f \u062e\u0637\u0648\u0627\u062a \u0644\u0644\u062a\u0631\u0627\u062c\u0639 \u0639\u0646\u0647\u0627'); if(g) g.textContent=''; return; }
    const r=await api('undoStep',S.tok,st[0].key,scope); toast('\u062a\u0631\u0627\u062c\u0639: '+r.reverted+' \u062e\u0644\u064a\u0629 ('+st[0].time.slice(11)+')'); await afterUndo(scope); }
  catch(e){ toast(e.message); } finally{ undoBusy=false; } }
document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{ if(!(e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey)||!(e.key.toLowerCase()==='z'||e.code==='KeyZ')) return; if(!$('#tbox')||document.activeElement===$('#tbox')) return; const t=document.activeElement; if(t&&/INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT/.test(t.tagName)) return; if($('#mdl')) return; e.preventDefault(); (S.gundo||undoLast)(); });
function undoForm(scope, LB){ scope=scope||''; LB=LB||lbl;

  const load=async()=>{ try{ const st=await api('listSteps',S.tok,scope);
    $('#steps').className=''; $('#steps').innerHTML=st.length?`<div class="tw" style="max-height:55vh"><table><thead><tr><th>\u0627\u0644\u0648\u0642\u062a</th><th>\u0627\u0644\u062e\u0644\u0627\u064a\u0627</th><th>\u0627\u0644\u062d\u0642\u0648\u0644</th><th>\u0645\u062b\u0627\u0644</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>`+st.map((x,i)=>`<tr><td>${esc(x.time.slice(11))}<div class="muted">${esc(x.time.slice(0,10))}</div></td><td>${x.count} \u062e\u0644\u064a\u0629 / ${x.orders} ${scope?'\u0635\u0641':'\u0623\u0645\u0631'}</td><td>${Object.entries(x.byField).map(([k,n])=>esc(LB(k))+' ('+n+')').join('\u060c ')}</td><td class="muted" style="white-space:normal;max-width:260px">${x.sample.map(esc).join('<br>')}</td><td><button class="${i===0?'pri':''}" data-k="${x.key}">\u062a\u0631\u0627\u062c\u0639</button></td></tr>`).join('')+'</tbody></table></div>':'\u0644\u0627 \u062a\u0648\u062c\u062f \u062e\u0637\u0648\u0627\u062a \u0644\u0643 \u064a\u0645\u0643\u0646 \u0627\u0644\u062a\u0631\u0627\u062c\u0639 \u0639\u0646\u0647\u0627.';
    document.querySelectorAll('#steps [data-k]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=async()=>{ b.disabled=true; b.textContent='\u2026';
      try{ const r=await api('undoStep',S.tok,b.dataset.k,scope); toast('\u062a\u0645 \u0627\u0644\u062a\u0631\u0627\u062c\u0639 \u0639\u0646 '+r.reverted+' \u062e\u0644\u064a\u0629'); await afterUndo(scope); undoForm(scope,LB); }catch(e){ $('#uerr2').textContent=e.message; b.disabled=false; } }); }
   catch(e){ $('#uerr2').textContent=e.message; } };
  load();
}

function bulkEdit(list){
  if(!list.length) return toast('\u0644\u0627 \u062a\u0648\u062c\u062f \u0623\u0648\u0627\u0645\u0631 \u0641\u064a \u0627\u0644\u0642\u0627\u0626\u0645\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u062d\u0627\u0644\u064a\u0629');
  const L=S.B.lists, ed=S.B.editable, names=S.B.people.map(p=>p.name);
  const cnt=k=>{const m={};list.forEach(o=>{const v=o[k]||'\u2014';m[v]=(m[v]||0)+1;});return Object.entries(m).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).map(([k,n])=>`${esc(k)} (${n})`).join('\u060c ');};
  const f=(k,html)=>ed.includes(k)?`<label>${LBL[k]}${html}</label>`:'';
  const opt=(arr)=>`<option value="">\u2014 \u0628\u062f\u0648\u0646 \u062a\u063a\u064a\u064a\u0631 \u2014</option>`+arr.map(x=>`<option>${esc(x)}</option>`).join('');
  modal(`<h3 style="margin-top:0">\u062a\u0639\u062f\u064a\u0644 \u062c\u0645\u0627\u0639\u064a \u0639\u0644\u0649 ${list.length} \u0623\u0645\u0631\u0627\u064b</h3>
   <div class="card" style="margin-bottom:10px"><div class="muted">\u0627\u0644\u062a\u0639\u062f\u064a\u0644 \u064a\u064f\u0637\u0628\u064e\u0651\u0642 \u0639\u0644\u0649 \u0643\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0648\u0627\u0645\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0638\u0627\u0647\u0631\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u0622\u0646 \u062d\u0633\u0628 \u0627\u0644\u0641\u0644\u0627\u062a\u0631.</div>
    <div><b>\u0627\u0644\u0645\u0643\u0627\u062a\u0628:</b> ${cnt('office')}</div><div><b>\u0627\u0644\u0645\u0631\u0627\u062d\u0644:</b> ${cnt('cur_stage')}</div><div><b>\u0627\u0644\u0645\u0633\u0624\u0648\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u062d\u0627\u0644\u064a:</b> ${cnt('cur_owner')}</div></div>
   <div class="f">${f('cur_owner',`<select id="b_cur_owner">${opt(names)}</select>`)}${f('stage',`<select id="b_stage"><option value="">\u2014 \u0628\u062f\u0648\u0646 \u062a\u063a\u064a\u064a\u0631 \u2014</option>${stNames().map(x=>`<option value="${esc(x)}">${esc(stLbl(x))}</option>`).join('')}</select>`)}
    ${f('next_action','<input id="b_next_action" placeholder="\u0627\u062a\u0631\u0643\u0647 \u0641\u0627\u0631\u063a\u0627\u064b \u0644\u0639\u062f\u0645 \u0627\u0644\u062a\u063a\u064a\u064a\u0631">')}${f('followup_date','<input id="b_followup_date" type="date">')}
    ${f('blocker','<input id="b_blocker" placeholder="\u0627\u062a\u0631\u0643\u0647 \u0641\u0627\u0631\u063a\u0627\u064b \u0644\u0639\u062f\u0645 \u0627\u0644\u062a\u063a\u064a\u064a\u0631">')}${f('blocker_owner',`<select id="b_blocker_owner">${opt(L.blocker_owner||[])}</select>`)}
    ${f('site_engineer',`<select id="b_site_engineer">${opt(names)}</select>`)}<label>\u0645\u0644\u0627\u062d\u0638\u0629 \u0639\u0644\u0649 \u0627\u0644\u0633\u062c\u0644<input id="b_note" placeholder="\u0645\u062b\u0627\u0644: \u062a\u0648\u0632\u064a\u0639 \u0645\u0633\u0624\u0648\u0644\u064a\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u062e\u0628\u0631"></label></div>
   <div class="err" id="berr"></div><button class="pri" id="bgo">\u062a\u0637\u0628\u064a\u0642 \u0639\u0644\u0649 ${list.length} \u0623\u0645\u0631\u0627\u064b</button> <button onclick="closeModal()">\u0625\u0644\u063a\u0627\u0621</button>`);
  $('#bgo').onclick=async()=>{
    const ch={}; BULK.forEach(k=>{const el=$('#b_'+k); if(el&&el.value.trim()) ch[k]=el.value.trim();});
    if(!Object.keys(ch).length) return $('#berr').textContent='\u0627\u062e\u062a\u0631 \u062d\u0642\u0644\u0627\u064b \u0648\u0627\u062d\u062f\u0627\u064b \u0639\u0644\u0649 \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0642\u0644';
    const summary=Object.keys(ch).map(k=>LBL[k]+': '+ch[k]).join(' \u060c ');
    if(!confirm(`\u0633\u064a\u062a\u0645 \u062a\u0639\u062f\u064a\u0644 ${list.length} \u0623\u0645\u0631\u0627\u064b:\n${summary}\n\n\u0645\u062a\u0627\u0628\u0639\u0629\u061f`)) return;
    ch._note=$('#b_note').value.trim(); $('#bgo').disabled=true; $('#bgo').textContent='\u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u0627\u0644\u062d\u0641\u0638\u2026';
    try{
      const r=await api('bulkSave',S.tok,list.map(o=>o.key),ch);
      const t=S.B.today; list.forEach(o=>{ Object.keys(ch).forEach(k=>{ if(k[0]!=='_') o[k]=ch[k]; });
        if(ch.stage){ o.cur_stage=ch.stage; o.open=(S.pct[o.cur_stage]??0)<100; o.exec=(S.pct[o.cur_stage]??0)<90; }
        if(ch.stage||ch.cur_owner) o.cur_since=t; });
      closeModal(); toast(`\u062a\u0645 \u062a\u0639\u062f\u064a\u0644 ${r.done} \u0623\u0645\u0631\u0627\u064b`+(r.skipped?` \u00b7 ${r.skipped} \u062e\u0627\u0631\u062c \u0635\u0644\u0627\u062d\u064a\u062a\u0643`:'')); shell();
    }catch(e){ $('#berr').textContent=e.message; $('#bgo').disabled=false; $('#bgo').textContent='\u062a\u0637\u0628\u064a\u0642'; }
  };
}

function vMine(){
  const me=S.B.user.name, t=S.B.today;
  const mine=S.orders.filter(o=>(o.site_engineer===me && (!needsQF(o) || !o.qf_id || o.qf_status==='\u0645\u0631\u0641\u0648\u0636')) || o.cur_owner===me || String(o.qd_rej||'').split(',').map(x=>x.trim()).includes(me) || (o.followup_date && o.followup_date<=t && o.open) || (o.qf_status==='\u0645\u0631\u0641\u0648\u0636' && [o.site_engineer,o.engineer].includes(me)) || (S.B.isQE && o.qf_status==='\u0628\u0627\u0646\u062a\u0638\u0627\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u062c\u0648\u062f\u0629'));
  sheet(mine, ['wo','office','contractor','due_date','left','followup_date','next_action','blocker','blocker_owner','cur_stage','cur_owner','since','qf','vio','mom','site','boq','att'], {kpis:'<div id="mymt"></div>',intro:'\u0627\u0644\u0623\u0648\u0627\u0645\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0633\u0646\u062f\u0629 \u0625\u0644\u064a\u0643 \u062d\u0627\u0644\u064a\u0627\u064b\u060c \u0648\u0627\u0644\u0623\u0648\u0627\u0645\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u062a\u064a \u062d\u0644\u0651 \u0645\u0648\u0639\u062f \u0645\u062a\u0627\u0628\u0639\u062a\u0647\u0627. \u0645\u0647\u0646\u062f\u0633 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0648\u0642\u0639: \u0639\u0646\u062f \u0627\u0646\u062a\u0647\u0627\u0621 \u0627\u0644\u0639\u0645\u0644 \u063a\u064a\u0651\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0631\u062d\u0644\u0629 \u0625\u0644\u0649 \u00ab\u062a\u0645 \u0627\u0644\u062a\u0646\u0641\u064a\u0630\u00bb \u0648\u0627\u0631\u0641\u0639 \u0645\u0644\u0641 \u0627\u0644\u062c\u0648\u062f\u0629 \u0641\u064a\u062e\u062a\u0641\u064a \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0645\u0631 \u0645\u0646 \u0642\u0627\u0626\u0645\u062a\u0643. \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0648\u0627\u0645\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u062a\u064a \u062a\u062c\u0627\u0648\u0632\u062a \u0627\u0644\u062a\u0646\u0641\u064a\u0630 \u064a\u0643\u0641\u064a \u0641\u064a\u0647\u0627 \u0631\u0641\u0639 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0644\u0641.'});
  mineMaint();

    src:()=>'\u0627\u0644\u062c\u062f\u0648\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u064a\u0648\u0645\u064a', clr:()=>tc, chip:()=>'', cls:(o,k)=>k==='wo'?'wo':'',

    pre:`<label>\u0645\u0646 <input type="date" id="dd" value="${d}"></label><label>\u0625\u0644\u0649 <input type="date" id="dd2" value="${d2}"></label>`,
    bar:can?'<button class="pri" id="dadd">\u0625\u0636\u0627\u0641\u0629 \u0644\u0644\u062c\u062f\u0648\u0644</button><button id="ddel">\u062d\u0630\u0641 \u0627\u0644\u0635\u0641\u0648\u0641 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u062d\u062f\u062f\u0629</button><button id="dundo">\u21b6 \u062a\u0631\u0627\u062c\u0639</button>':'', hint:'\u060c \u0646\u0642\u0631\u062a\u0627\u0646 \u0639\u0644\u0649 \u0631\u0642\u0645 \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0645\u0631 \u0644\u0641\u062a\u062d \u0633\u062c\u0644\u0647',

  modal(`<h3 style="margin-top:0">\u0625\u0636\u0627\u0641\u0629 \u0644\u0644\u062c\u062f\u0648\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u064a\u0648\u0645\u064a</h3><div class="f">

   <button class="pri" id="xsave">\u062d\u0641\u0638</button> <button onclick="closeModal()">\u0625\u0644\u063a\u0627\u0621</button>`);
  $('#xsave').onclick=()=>{ const row={}; ['date','wo','office','work','time','engineer','phone','receiver','receiver_phone','station','location','note'].forEach(k=>row[k]=$('#x_'+k).value.trim());

async function openOrder(wo){
  modal('<div class="loading">\u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u0627\u0644\u062a\u062d\u0645\u064a\u0644\u2026</div>');
  let r; try { r=await api('orderDetail',S.tok,wo); } catch(e){ return modal(`<div class="err">${esc(e.message)}</div>`); }
  const o=r.order, ed=S.B.editable, L=S.B.lists;
  const inp=(k)=>{ if(!ed.includes(k)) return '';
    const opts={stage:stNames(), munib_reason:L.reason, blocker_owner:L.blocker_owner, cur_owner:S.B.people.map(p=>p.name), permit:['\u062a\u0645 \u0627\u0635\u062f\u0627\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u062a\u0635\u0631\u064a\u062d','\u0644\u0645 \u064a\u062a\u0645 \u0627\u0644\u0625\u0635\u062f\u0627\u0631','\u062a\u0635\u0631\u064a\u062d \u0645\u0646\u062a\u0647\u064a','\u0644\u0627 \u064a\u0646\u0637\u0628\u0642'], materials:['\u062a\u0645 \u0635\u0631\u0641 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0648\u0627\u062f','\u0635\u0631\u0641 \u062c\u0632\u0626\u064a','\u0644\u0645 \u064a\u062a\u0645 \u0627\u0644\u0635\u0631\u0641','\u0645\u0648\u0627\u062f \u063a\u064a\u0631 \u0645\u062a\u0648\u0641\u0631\u0647']}[k];
    const val=k==='stage'?o.cur_stage||o.stage:o[k];
    const ctl= opts ? `<select data-k="${k}"><option value=""></option>${opts.map(x=>`<option value="${esc(x)}" ${x===val?'selected':''}>${esc(k==='stage'?stLbl(x):x)}</option>`).join('')}</select>`
      : k==='followup_date' ? `<input type="date" data-k="${k}" value="${esc(val)}">`
      : ['notes','munib_remarks','next_action','blocker'].includes(k) ? `<textarea data-k="${k}" rows="2">${esc(val)}</textarea>` : `<input data-k="${k}" value="${esc(val)}">`;
    return `<label>${LBL[k]}${ctl}</label>`; };
  const info=['type','ccode','office','district','contractor','consultant','assign_date','duration','due_date','engineer','site_engineer','site_date','oracle','last_action','last_action_date','olap_holder','po','cost','updated_at','updated_by'];

     <div class="card" style="margin-top:10px"><div class="bar" style="margin:0"><span class="muted">\u0627\u0644\u062a\u0628\u0648\u064a\u0628\u0627\u062a \u0627\u0644\u062a\u064a \u064a\u0631\u0627\u0647\u0627</span><span id="utm" class="pill"></span><span class="sp"></span><button id="utauto" type="button">\u0625\u0631\u062c\u0627\u0639 \u0625\u0644\u0649 \u0627\u0644\u062a\u0644\u0642\u0627\u0626\u064a \u062d\u0633\u0628 \u0627\u0644\u062f\u0648\u0631</button></div>

    modal(`<h3 style="margin-top:0">\u062d\u0630\u0641 \u0645\u0648\u0638\u0641</h3><p>\u0633\u064a\u064f\u062d\u0630\u0641 <b>${esc(x.name)}</b> (${x.id}) \u0645\u0646 \u0627\u0644\u0628\u0631\u0646\u0627\u0645\u062c \u0648\u0644\u0646 \u064a\u0633\u062a\u0637\u064a\u0639 \u0627\u0644\u062f\u062e\u0648\u0644.</p><p class="muted">\u0633\u062c\u0644 \u062a\u0639\u062f\u064a\u0644\u0627\u062a\u0647 \u0627\u0644\u0633\u0627\u0628\u0642\u0629 \u0639\u0644\u0649 \u0623\u0648\u0627\u0645\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0639\u0645\u0644 \u064a\u0628\u0642\u0649 \u0645\u062d\u0641\u0648\u0638\u0627\u064b. \u0625\u0646 \u0643\u0627\u0646 \u0633\u064a\u0639\u0648\u062f \u0644\u0627\u062d\u0642\u0627\u064b \u0641\u0627\u0644\u0623\u0641\u0636\u0644 \u062c\u0639\u0644\u0647 \u00ab\u063a\u064a\u0631 \u0645\u0641\u0639\u0651\u0644\u00bb \u0628\u062f\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u062d\u0630\u0641.</p>
      <div class="err" id="derr"></div><button class="pri" id="dyes" style="background:var(--dan);border-color:var(--dan)">\u062a\u0623\u0643\u064a\u062f \u0627\u0644\u062d\u0630\u0641</button> <button onclick="closeModal()">\u0625\u0644\u063a\u0627\u0621</button>`);
    $('#dyes').onclick=()=>{ $('#dyes').disabled=true; api('deleteUser',S.tok,x.id).then(()=>{closeModal();toast('\u062a\u0645 \u062d\u0630\u0641 '+x.name);vUsers();}).catch(er=>{$('#derr').textContent=er.message;$('#dyes').disabled=false;}); }; });
}

function xlRich(c){ const col=h=>{ const x=String(h||'').replace(/^#/,'').replace(/^FF/i,'').toUpperCase(); return Object.keys(RICH_C).find(n=>RICH_C[n].slice(1).toUpperCase()===x)||''; }, dec=t=>String(t).replace(new RegExp('<br\\s{0,}\\/{0,1}>','g'),'\n').replace(/<[^>]+>/g,'').replace(/&lt;/g,'<').replace(/&gt;/g,'>').replace(/&quot;/g,'"').replace(/&#39;/g,"'").replace(/&amp;/g,'&');
  const wrap=(n,t)=>n&&t?'['+n+']'+t+'[/'+n+']':t; let out='', any=false;
  if(typeof c.r==='string'&&c.r.indexOf('<r')>=0){ const re=new RegExp('<r>([\\s\\S]*?)<\\/r>','g'); let m; while((m=re.exec(c.r))){ const seg=m[1], cm=new RegExp('<color[^>]*rgb="([0-9A-Fa-f]+)"').exec(seg), tm=new RegExp('<t[^>]*>([\\s\\S]*?)<\\/t>','g'); let t='', x; while((x=tm.exec(seg))) t+=dec(x[1]); const n=cm?col(cm[1]):''; if(n) any=true; out+=wrap(n,t); } return any?out:null; }
  if(typeof c.h==='string'&&c.h.indexOf('<span')>=0){ const re=new RegExp('<span([^>]*)>([\\s\\S]*?)<\\/span>|([^<]+)|(<br\\s{0,}\\/{0,1}>)','g'); let m; while((m=re.exec(c.h))){ if(m[4]){ out+='\n'; continue; } if(m[3]!=null){ out+=dec(m[3]); continue; } const cm=new RegExp('color:\\s{0,}#?([0-9A-Fa-f]{6})','i').exec(m[1]||''), n=cm?col(cm[1]):''; if(n) any=true; out+=wrap(n,dec(m[2])); } return any?out:null; }
  return null; }
function uniPack(wb){ const p={}, info=[]; let n=0; const inv=M=>{ const r={}; Object.keys(M).forEach(k=>r[M[k]]=k); return r; }; const LI=inv(LBL), MI=inv(ML), DI=inv(DL), PI=inv(PL);
  const S2=x=>String(x==null?'':x).replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim(), isDate=x=>x instanceof Date&&!isNaN(x), ymd=d=>{ const p=n=>String(n).padStart(2,'0'); return d.getFullYear()+'-'+p(d.getMonth()+1)+'-'+p(d.getDate()); }, cv=x=>isDate(x)?ymd(x):(typeof x==='number'?String(x):S2(x));
  const DK=['assign_date','due_date','followup_date','site_date','cur_since','munib_permit_date','date','start_date','end_date','last_action_date','qf_at'], serial=n=>new Date(Math.round((n-25569)*864e5)).toISOString().slice(0,10);
  const offs=(S.B.lists.office||[]).map(S2).concat(['\u0628\u0644\u0627 \u0645\u0643\u062a\u0628']), names=wb.SheetNames.map(S2), hasOff=names.some(x=>offs.includes(x)&&!UNI_FIXED.includes(x)), skipped=[];
  wb.SheetNames.forEach(name=>{ const nm=S2(name); let spec=UNI_SHEETS[nm]; if(!spec&&offs.includes(nm)&&!UNI_FIXED.includes(nm)) spec=UNI_SHEETS['\u0627\u0644\u0625\u0633\u0646\u0627\u062f\u0627\u062a']; if(!spec) return; if(nm==='\u0627\u0644\u0625\u0633\u0646\u0627\u062f\u0627\u062a'&&hasOff){ skipped.push(nm); return; }
    const ws=wb.Sheets[name], rows=XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(ws,{header:1,raw:true,defval:''}); if(rows.length<3) return;
    const bands=rows[0].map(S2), labels=rows[1].map(S2), sec=spec[0], own=spec[1], map=sec==='orders'?LI:sec==='maint'?MI:sec==='daily'?DI:PI; const cols=[];
    labels.forEach((lb,i)=>{ if(i===0) return; let k=map[lb]; if(!k&&lb==='\u0645') k='id'; if(!k) return; if(own&&!own.includes(bands[i])) return; if(k==='id'||(k==='wo'&&sec==='daily')) return; cols.push([i,k]); });
    if(!cols.length) return; const idCol=sec==='orders'?0:labels.indexOf('\u0645'); if(sec!=='orders'&&idCol<0) return; const recs=[]; const ec=XLSX.utils&&XLSX.utils.encode_cell;
    rows.slice(2).forEach((r,ri)=>{ const key=cv(r[idCol]); if(!key) return; const f={}; cols.forEach(([i,k])=>{ let v=cv(r[i]); if(DK.includes(k)&&typeof r[i]==='number'&&r[i]>20000&&r[i]<80000) v=serial(r[i]); if(v&&ec&&typeof r[i]==='string'){ const c=ws[ec({r:ri+2,c:i})]; if(c&&(c.r||c.h)){ const t=xlRich(c); if(t!=null) v=S2(t); } } f[k]=v; }); recs.push(sec==='orders'?{key,f}:{id:key,f}); });
    if(!recs.length) return; (p[sec]=p[sec]||[]).push(...recs); n+=recs.length; info.push(nm+' ('+recs.length+' \u0635\u0641\u0627\u064b\u060c '+cols.length+' \u0639\u0645\u0648\u062f\u0627\u064b)'); });
  if(skipped.length) info.push('\u062a\u064f\u0631\u0643\u062a \u0648\u0631\u0642\u0629 \u00ab\u0627\u0644\u0625\u0633\u0646\u0627\u062f\u0627\u062a\u00bb \u0627\u0644\u0645\u062c\u0645\u064e\u0651\u0639\u0629 \u0644\u0623\u0646 \u0623\u0648\u0631\u0627\u0642 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0643\u0627\u062a\u0628 \u0645\u0648\u062c\u0648\u062f\u0629');
  return {p,n,info}; }

  return `<div><b>\u0627\u0644\u0645\u0644\u0641 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0648\u062d\u0651\u062f</b> \u2014 \u0642\u064f\u0631\u0626\u062a: ${esc(pack.info.join('\u060c '))}</div>`+sec('\u0627\u0644\u0623\u0648\u0627\u0645\u0631 (\u0627\u0644\u0625\u0633\u0646\u0627\u062f\u0627\u062a\u060c \u0627\u0644\u0645\u062a\u0639\u062b\u0631\u0627\u062a\u060c \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0637\u0627\u0628\u0642\u0629)',r.orders,'\u0623\u0645\u0631\u0627\u064b')+sec('\u0627\u0644\u0635\u064a\u0627\u0646\u0629 \u0648\u0627\u0644\u0637\u0648\u0627\u0631\u0626',r.maint,'\u0625\u0634\u0639\u0627\u0631\u0627\u064b')+sec('\u0627\u0644\u062c\u062f\u0648\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u064a\u0648\u0645\u064a',r.daily,'\u0635\u0641\u0627\u064b')+sec('\u0627\u0644\u062a\u0635\u0627\u0631\u064a\u062d',r.permits,'\u0637\u0644\u0628\u0627\u064b')+(r.warn&&r.warn.length?`<div class="card" style="margin-top:8px;border-color:var(--warn);color:var(--warn)">${r.warn.map(esc).join('<br>')}</div>`:''); }

  modal(`<div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap"><h3 style="margin:0">${G.t} \u2014 ${o.maint?'\u0625\u0634\u0639\u0627\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0635\u064a\u0627\u0646\u0629 D9 '+esc(o.wo):'\u0623\u0645\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0639\u0645\u0644 '+esc(o.wo)+(o.type?'-'+esc(o.type):'')}</h3><span class="sp"></span>${G.kinds.map(k=>`<button class="pri" data-new="${k}">+ ${FK[k].t}</button>`).join('')}<button onclick="closeModal()">\u0625\u063a\u0644\u0627\u0642</button></div>

   ${list.length?`<div class="tw" style="max-height:60vh"><table><thead><tr><th>\u0627\u0644\u0646\u0645\u0648\u0630\u062c</th><th>\u0627\u0644\u062a\u0627\u0631\u064a\u062e</th><th>${g==='vio'?'\u0627\u0644\u0645\u062e\u0627\u0644\u0641\u0629':g==='site'?'\u0627\u0644\u0645\u0644\u0627\u062d\u0638\u0627\u062a':'\u0627\u0644\u0645\u062d\u0627\u0648\u0631'}</th>${g==='vio'?'<th>\u0627\u0644\u063a\u0631\u0627\u0645\u0629</th>':''}<th>\u0627\u0644\u0643\u0627\u062a\u0628</th><th>\u0635\u0648\u0631</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>${list.map(f=>`<tr><td>${FK[f.kind].i} ${esc(FK[f.kind].t)} <b>${esc(f.seq)}</b></td><td>${esc(f.date)}</td><td class="wrap"><span>${esc(fmSum(f))}</span></td>${g==='vio'?`<td>${money(f.total)}</td>`:''}<td>${esc(f.author)}</td><td>${f.photos.length||''}</td><td style="white-space:nowrap"><button data-open="${esc(f.id)}">${f.can?'\u0641\u062a\u062d / \u062a\u0639\u062f\u064a\u0644':'\u0639\u0631\u0636'}</button> <button data-prt="${esc(f.id)}">\ud83d\udda8 \u0637\u0628\u0627\u0639\u0629</button></td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>${g==='vio'&&tot?`<div style="margin-top:6px">\u0625\u062c\u0645\u0627\u0644\u064a \u0627\u0644\u063a\u0631\u0627\u0645\u0627\u062a: <b>${money(tot)}</b> \u0631\u064a\u0627\u0644</div>`:''}`:`<div class="card muted" style="margin:8px 0">\u0644\u0627 \u062a\u0648\u062c\u062f ${G.t} \u0644\u0647\u0630\u0627 \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0645\u0631 \u0628\u0639\u062f.</div>`}`);
  const back=()=>formsModal(wo,g);
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-new]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>formEditor({kind:b.dataset.new,wo:o.key},o,back));
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-open]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>formEditor(list.find(f=>f.id===b.dataset.open),o,back));
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-prt]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>printForm(list.find(f=>f.id===b.dataset.prt),o));
}

const fileB64=f=>new Promise((ok,no)=>{ const r=new FileReader(); r.onload=()=>ok(String(r.result).split(',')[1]); r.onerror=()=>no(new Error('\u062a\u0639\u0630\u0651\u0631 \u0642\u0631\u0627\u0621\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0644\u0641: '+f.name)); r.readAsDataURL(f); });
const fsize=n=>n>1048576?(n/1048576).toFixed(1)+' \u0645.\u0628':n>1024?Math.round(n/1024)+' \u0643.\u0628':n+' \u0628\u0627\u064a\u062a';
async function attModal(key){
  modal('<div class="loading">\u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u0641\u062a\u062d \u0645\u062c\u0644\u062f \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0645\u0631\u2026</div>');
  let R; try{ R=await api('orderFiles',S.tok,key); }catch(e){ modal(`<div class="err">${esc(e.message)}</div><button onclick="closeModal()">\u0625\u063a\u0644\u0627\u0642</button>`); return; }
  const o=byRef(R.key||key); if(o) o.folder_id=R.folder_id; let busy=0;
  const draw=()=>{ modal(`<div style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap"><h3 style="margin:0">\ud83d\udcc1 ${/^D9:/.test(key)?'\u0645\u0631\u0641\u0642\u0627\u062a \u0625\u0634\u0639\u0627\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0635\u064a\u0627\u0646\u0629 '+esc(key.slice(3)):'\u0645\u0631\u0641\u0642\u0627\u062a \u0623\u0645\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0639\u0645\u0644 '+esc(R.key||key)}</h3><span class="sp"></span><a class="qfopen" href="${esc(R.folder)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">\u0641\u062a\u062d \u0627\u0644\u0645\u062c\u0644\u062f \u0641\u064a Drive</a><button onclick="closeModal()">\u0625\u063a\u0644\u0627\u0642</button></div>
     <div class="muted" style="margin:8px 0">${/^D9:/.test(key)?'\u0643\u0644 \u0645\u0627 \u064a\u062e\u0635 \u0627\u0644\u0625\u0634\u0639\u0627\u0631 \u0641\u064a \u0645\u062c\u0644\u062f \u0648\u0627\u062d\u062f: \u0645\u0644\u0641 \u0627\u0644\u062c\u0648\u062f\u0629 \u0648\u0635\u0648\u0631\u0647\u060c \u0648\u0623\u064a \u0645\u0644\u0641 \u062a\u0636\u064a\u0641\u0647 \u0647\u0646\u0627.':'\u0643\u0644 \u0645\u0627 \u064a\u062e\u0635 \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0645\u0631 \u0641\u064a \u0645\u062c\u0644\u062f \u0648\u0627\u062d\u062f: \u0645\u0644\u0641 \u0627\u0644\u062c\u0648\u062f\u0629\u060c \u0635\u0648\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u062e\u0627\u0644\u0641\u0627\u062a \u0648\u0627\u0644\u0645\u062d\u0627\u0636\u0631\u060c \u0648\u0623\u064a \u0645\u0644\u0641 \u062a\u0636\u064a\u0641\u0647 \u0647\u0646\u0627.'}</div>
     <div class="bar"><label class="upl">\ud83d\udcf7 \u062a\u0635\u0648\u064a\u0631 \u0628\u0627\u0644\u0643\u0627\u0645\u064a\u0631\u0627<input type="file" id="atc" data-img="1" data-cam="1" style="display:none"></label><label class="upl">\uff0b \u0625\u0636\u0627\u0641\u0629 \u0645\u0644\u0641\u0627\u062a<input type="file" id="atf" multiple style="display:none"></label><span id="atp" class="muted"></span></div>
     ${R.files.length?`<div class="tw" style="max-height:55vh"><table><thead><tr><th>\u0627\u0644\u0645\u0644\u0641</th><th>\u0627\u0644\u062d\u062c\u0645</th><th>\u0627\u0644\u062a\u0627\u0631\u064a\u062e</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>${R.files.map(x=>`<tr><td><a href="${esc(x.url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${esc(x.name)}</a></td><td>${fsize(+x.size||0)}</td><td>${esc(x.at)}</td><td>${R.canDelete?`<button data-atd="${esc(x.id)}" style="color:var(--dan);border-color:var(--dan)">\u062d\u0630\u0641</button>`:''}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`:'<div class="card muted">\u0644\u0627 \u062a\u0648\u062c\u062f \u0645\u0631\u0641\u0642\u0627\u062a \u0628\u0639\u062f.</div>'}
     <div class="err" id="aterr"></div>`);
    const send=async(b64,mime,name,loc)=>{ const x=await api('uploadAttachment',S.tok,key,b64,mime,name,loc||''); R.files.unshift(x); };
    const many=async fs=>{ if(busy||!fs.length) return; busy=1; let done=0, fail=0; $('#atp').textContent='\u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u0627\u0644\u0631\u0641\u0639\u2026 0/'+fs.length;
      for(const f of fs){ try{ if(f.size>30*1048576) throw new Error(f.name+': \u0623\u0643\u0628\u0631 \u0645\u0646 30 \u0645\u064a\u063a\u0627\u0628\u0627\u064a\u062a'); await send(await fileB64(f),f.type,f.name); }catch(e){ fail++; toast(e.message); } done++; if($('#atp')) $('#atp').textContent='\u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u0627\u0644\u0631\u0641\u0639\u2026 '+done+'/'+fs.length; }
      busy=0; if(fail) toast(fail+' \u0645\u0644\u0641\u0627\u064b \u0644\u0645 \u064a\u064f\u0631\u0641\u0639'); if($('#mdl')) draw(); };
    $('#atf').onchange=()=>{ const fs=[...$('#atf').files]; $('#atf').value=''; many(fs); };
    $('#atc').onchange=async()=>{ const f=$('#atc').files[0]; $('#atc').value=''; if(!f||busy) return; const sc=stampCtx(key); let im; try{ im=await cropImg(f); }catch(e){ return toast(e.message); } if(!im) return;
      busy=1; $('#atp').textContent='\u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u062a\u062d\u062f\u064a\u062f \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0648\u0642\u0639 \u0648\u062e\u062a\u0645 \u0627\u0644\u0635\u0648\u0631\u0629\u2026'; try{ im=await stampPhoto(im,sc); if($('#atp')) $('#atp').textContent='\u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u0631\u0641\u0639 \u0627\u0644\u0635\u0648\u0631\u0629\u2026'; await send(im.b64,im.mime,'\u0635\u0648\u0631\u0629_'+S.B.today+'_'+String(Date.now()).slice(-5)+'.jpg',im.loc); }catch(e){ toast(e.message); } busy=0; if($('#mdl')) draw(); };
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-atd]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=async()=>{ const x=R.files.find(z=>z.id===b.dataset.atd); if(!confirm('\u062d\u0630\u0641 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0644\u0641 \u00ab'+x.name+'\u00bb \u0645\u0646 \u0645\u062c\u0644\u062f \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0645\u0631\u061f')) return; b.disabled=true;
      try{ await api('deleteAttachment',S.tok,key,x.id); R.files=R.files.filter(z=>z.id!==x.id); draw(); }catch(e){ $('#aterr').textContent=e.message; b.disabled=false; } }); };
  draw();
}
let folderJob=false;
async function folderLoop(fn, label, el){ if(folderJob) return toast('\u0639\u0645\u0644\u064a\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u062c\u0644\u062f\u0627\u062a \u062a\u0639\u0645\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u0622\u0646'); folderJob=true; let total=0, bad=0; const say=t=>{ const e=el&&$(el); if(e) e.textContent=t; };
  try{ for(let i=0;i<2000;i++){ const r=await api(fn,S.tok); total+=(r.made||r.moved||0); bad+=(r.failed||0); say(label+': \u062a\u0645 '+total+(bad?' \u00b7 \u062a\u0639\u0630\u0651\u0631 '+bad:'')+' \u00b7 \u0627\u0644\u0628\u0627\u0642\u064a '+r.left); if(!r.left||!((r.made||0)+(r.moved||0)+(r.failed||0))) break; }
    say(label+': \u0627\u0643\u062a\u0645\u0644 ('+total+')'+(bad?' \u00b7 \u062a\u0639\u0630\u0651\u0631 '+bad+' (\u0645\u0644\u0641\u0627\u062a \u0645\u062d\u0630\u0648\u0641\u0629 \u0623\u0648 \u0628\u0644\u0627 \u0623\u0645\u0631)':'')); toast(label+': \u0627\u0643\u062a\u0645\u0644 ('+total+')'); }
  catch(e){ say(label+': \u062a\u0648\u0642\u0651\u0641 \u2014 '+e.message+' (\u0627\u0636\u063a\u0637 \u0627\u0644\u0632\u0631 \u0645\u0631\u0629 \u0623\u062e\u0631\u0649 \u0644\u0644\u0645\u062a\u0627\u0628\u0639\u0629)'); toast(e.message); }
  folderJob=false; return total; }
function getLoc(){ return new Promise((ok,no)=>{ if(!navigator.geolocation) return no(new Error('\u0627\u0644\u0645\u062a\u0635\u0641\u062d \u0644\u0627 \u064a\u062f\u0639\u0645 \u062a\u062d\u062f\u064a\u062f \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0648\u0642\u0639'));
  const tm=setTimeout(()=>no(new Error('\u0644\u0645 \u064a\u0635\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0648\u0642\u0639 \u062e\u0644\u0627\u0644 40 \u062b\u0627\u0646\u064a\u0629. \u0648\u0627\u0641\u0642 \u0639\u0644\u0649 \u0637\u0644\u0628 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0648\u0642\u0639 \u0625\u0646 \u0638\u0647\u0631\u060c \u0648\u062a\u0623\u0643\u062f \u0645\u0646 \u062a\u0634\u063a\u064a\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0648\u0642\u0639 (GPS) \u0641\u064a \u0627\u0644\u062c\u0648\u0627\u0644')),40000);
  navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(p=>{ clearTimeout(tm); ok({lat:p.coords.latitude,lng:p.coords.longitude,acc:p.coords.accuracy}); },
    e=>{ clearTimeout(tm); no(new Error(e&&e.code===1?'\u0644\u0645 \u064a\u064f\u0633\u0645\u062d \u0644\u0644\u0628\u0631\u0646\u0627\u0645\u062c \u0628\u0645\u0639\u0631\u0641\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0648\u0642\u0639. \u0627\u0633\u0645\u062d \u0628\u0627\u0644\u0645\u0648\u0642\u0639 \u0644\u0647\u0630\u0647 \u0627\u0644\u0635\u0641\u062d\u0629 \u0645\u0646 \u0625\u0639\u062f\u0627\u062f\u0627\u062a \u0627\u0644\u0645\u062a\u0635\u0641\u062d':e&&e.code===3?'\u0627\u0646\u062a\u0647\u062a \u0645\u0647\u0644\u0629 \u062a\u062d\u062f\u064a\u062f \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0648\u0642\u0639. \u062a\u0623\u0643\u062f \u0645\u0646 \u062a\u0634\u063a\u064a\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0648\u0642\u0639 (GPS) \u0641\u064a \u0627\u0644\u062c\u0648\u0627\u0644':'\u062a\u0639\u0630\u0651\u0631 \u062a\u062d\u062f\u064a\u062f \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0648\u0642\u0639. \u062a\u0623\u0643\u062f \u0645\u0646 \u062a\u0634\u063a\u064a\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0648\u0642\u0639 (GPS) \u0648\u0627\u0644\u0625\u0646\u062a\u0631\u0646\u062a')); },
    {enableHighAccuracy:true,timeout:20000,maximumAge:30000}); }); }
function stampCtx(ref){ const c={info:api('stampInfo',S.tok,ref,!S.aji),loc:getLoc()}; c.info.catch(()=>{}); c.loc.catch(()=>{}); return c; }
function loadImg(src){ return new Promise((ok,no)=>{ const im=new Image(); im.onload=()=>ok(im); im.onerror=()=>no(new Error('\u062a\u0639\u0630\u0651\u0631 \u0642\u0631\u0627\u0621\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u0635\u0648\u0631\u0629')); im.src=src; }); }
async function stampPhoto(im,c){ const info=await c.info; if(info.logo) S.aji=info.logo; let loc=null;
  for(;;){ try{ loc=await c.loc; break; }catch(e){ if(!info.need) break; if(!confirm(e.message+'.\n\n\u0627\u0644\u0645\u0648\u0642\u0639 \u0645\u0637\u0644\u0648\u0628 \u0644\u0631\u0641\u0639 \u0635\u0648\u0631\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u0643\u0627\u0645\u064a\u0631\u0627. \u0625\u0639\u0627\u062f\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u062d\u0627\u0648\u0644\u0629\u061f')) throw new Error('\u0644\u0645 \u062a\u064f\u0631\u0641\u0639 \u0627\u0644\u0635\u0648\u0631\u0629: \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0648\u0642\u0639 \u0645\u0637\u0644\u0648\u0628'); c.loc=getLoc(); c.loc.catch(()=>{}); } }
  let cv=im.cv; if(!cv){ const src=await loadImg(im.du); cv=document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width=src.naturalWidth; cv.height=src.naturalHeight; cv.getContext('2d').drawImage(src,0,0); }
  const W=cv.width, H=cv.height, g=cv.getContext('2d'), m=Math.min(W,H), pad=Math.max(6,Math.round(m*0.025)), ltr=t=>'\u202a'+t+'\u202c';
  const ty=info.type?info.type+(info.ccode&&info.ccode!==info.type?' ('+info.ccode+')':''):(info.ccode||'');
  const groups=[[ltr(info.at),'\u0627\u0644\u0645\u0647\u0646\u062f\u0633: '+info.by],['\u0623\u0645\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0639\u0645\u0644 '+ltr(info.wo)+(ty?' \u00b7 \u0627\u0644\u0646\u0648\u0639 '+ltr(ty):''),info.district?'\u0627\u0644\u062d\u064a: '+info.district:''],['\u0627\u0644\u0645\u0648\u0642\u0639: '+(loc?ltr(loc.lat.toFixed(6)+', '+loc.lng.toFixed(6))+(loc.acc?' (\u00b1'+Math.round(loc.acc)+' \u0645)':''):'\u063a\u064a\u0631 \u0645\u062a\u0627\u062d')]];
  let fs=Math.max(12,Math.round(W/40)); const font=()=>{ g.font='600 '+fs+'px Tahoma, Arial, sans-serif'; }; font(); const lines=[];
  groups.forEach(gr=>{ gr=gr.filter(Boolean); const j=gr.join(' \u00b7 '); if(gr.length<2||g.measureText(j).width<=W-2*pad) lines.push(j); else gr.forEach(t=>lines.push(t)); });
  while(fs>10&&Math.max(...lines.map(t=>g.measureText(t).width))>W-2*pad){ fs--; font(); }
  const lh=Math.round(fs*1.5), sh=lh*lines.length+pad, oc=document.createElement('canvas'); oc.width=W; oc.height=H+sh; const q=oc.getContext('2d'); q.drawImage(cv,0,0); q.fillStyle='#1f2d3d'; q.fillRect(0,H,W,sh);
  q.font=g.font; q.fillStyle='#fff'; q.textBaseline='middle'; q.direction='rtl'; q.textAlign='right'; lines.forEach((t,i)=>q.fillText(t,W-pad,H+pad/2+lh*i+lh/2,W-2*pad));
  if(S.aji){ try{ const lg=await loadImg(S.aji), L=Math.max(40,Math.round(m*0.16)); q.drawImage(lg,pad,pad,L,L); }catch(e){} }
  const du=oc.toDataURL('image/jpeg',0.82); return {du,b64:du.split(',')[1],mime:'image/jpeg',st:1,loc:loc?loc.lat.toFixed(7)+','+loc.lng.toFixed(7):'-'}; }
function cropImg(src){ return new Promise((ok,no)=>{ const isF=typeof src!=='string', url=isF?URL.createObjectURL(src):src, im=new Image();
  im.onerror=()=>{ if(isF) URL.revokeObjectURL(url); no(new Error('\u062a\u0639\u0630\u0651\u0631 \u0642\u0631\u0627\u0621\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u0635\u0648\u0631\u0629')); };
  im.onload=()=>{ const sc=Math.min(1,1600/Math.max(im.naturalWidth,im.naturalHeight)); let work=document.createElement('canvas'); work.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(im.naturalWidth*sc)); work.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(im.naturalHeight*sc));
    let g=work.getContext('2d'); g.fillStyle='#fff'; g.fillRect(0,0,work.width,work.height); g.drawImage(im,0,0,work.width,work.height); if(isF) URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    const ov=document.createElement('div'); ov.className='crop'; ov.id='crop';
    ov.innerHTML='<div class="cmsg">\u0627\u0633\u062d\u0628 \u0627\u0644\u0625\u0637\u0627\u0631 \u0623\u0648 \u0623\u0631\u0643\u0627\u0646\u0647 \u0644\u062a\u062d\u062f\u064a\u062f \u0627\u0644\u062c\u0632\u0621 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0637\u0644\u0648\u0628</div><div class="stg" id="cstg"><canvas id="ccv"></canvas><div class="rc" id="crc"><span class="hd" data-h="tl" style="top:-15px;left:-15px"></span><span class="hd" data-h="tr" style="top:-15px;right:-15px"></span><span class="hd" data-h="bl" style="bottom:-15px;left:-15px"></span><span class="hd" data-h="br" style="bottom:-15px;right:-15px"></span></div></div><div class="cbar"><button id="crot">\u21bb \u062a\u062f\u0648\u064a\u0631</button><button class="pri" id="cok">\u0642\u0635\u0651 \u0648\u0631\u0641\u0639</button><button id="cas">\u0631\u0641\u0639 \u0643\u0645\u0627 \u0647\u064a</button><button id="cno">\u0625\u0644\u063a\u0627\u0621</button></div>';

    const put=()=>{ rc.style.left=R.x+'px'; rc.style.top=R.y+'px'; rc.style.width=R.w+'px'; rc.style.height=R.h+'px'; };
    const fit=()=>{ k=Math.min(1,(window.innerWidth-40)/work.width,(window.innerHeight-170)/work.height); cv.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(work.width*k)); cv.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(work.height*k)); cv.getContext('2d').drawImage(work,0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
      R={x:Math.round(cv.width*0.05),y:Math.round(cv.height*0.05),w:Math.round(cv.width*0.9),h:Math.round(cv.height*0.9)}; put(); };
    fit(); let drag=null;
    const clamp=()=>{ R.w=Math.max(24,Math.min(R.w,cv.width)); R.h=Math.max(24,Math.min(R.h,cv.height)); R.x=Math.max(0,Math.min(R.x,cv.width-R.w)); R.y=Math.max(0,Math.min(R.y,cv.height-R.h)); };
    stg.onpointerdown=e=>{ e.preventDefault(); const b=stg.getBoundingClientRect(), px=e.clientX-b.left, py=e.clientY-b.top, h=e.target.dataset&&e.target.dataset.h;
      const inside=px>=R.x&&px<=R.x+R.w&&py>=R.y&&py<=R.y+R.h; drag={m:h||(inside?'mv':'new'),px,py,R0:Object.assign({},R)}; if(drag.m==='new'){ R={x:px,y:py,w:24,h:24}; drag.R0=Object.assign({},R); drag.m='br'; put(); } try{ stg.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); }catch(er){} };
    stg.onpointermove=e=>{ if(!drag) return; const b=stg.getBoundingClientRect(), dx=e.clientX-b.left-drag.px, dy=e.clientY-b.top-drag.py, o=drag.R0, m=drag.m;
      if(m==='mv'){ R.x=o.x+dx; R.y=o.y+dy; } else { let x1=o.x, y1=o.y, x2=o.x+o.w, y2=o.y+o.h; if(m==='tl'||m==='bl') x1=Math.min(x2-24,Math.max(0,o.x+dx)); if(m==='tr'||m==='br') x2=Math.max(x1+24,Math.min(cv.width,o.x+o.w+dx)); if(m==='tl'||m==='tr') y1=Math.min(y2-24,Math.max(0,o.y+dy)); if(m==='bl'||m==='br') y2=Math.max(y1+24,Math.min(cv.height,o.y+o.h+dy)); R={x:x1,y:y1,w:x2-x1,h:y2-y1}; }
      clamp(); put(); };
    stg.onpointerup=stg.onpointercancel=()=>{ drag=null; };
    const out=(x,y,w,h)=>{ const c=document.createElement('canvas'); c.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(w)); c.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(h)); const q=c.getContext('2d'); q.fillStyle='#fff'; q.fillRect(0,0,c.width,c.height); q.drawImage(work,x,y,w,h,0,0,c.width,c.height); const du=c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',0.8); return {du,b64:du.split(',')[1],mime:'image/jpeg',cv:c}; };
    const end=v=>{ ov.remove(); ok(v); };
    ov.querySelector('#crot').onclick=()=>{ const c=document.createElement('canvas'); c.width=work.height; c.height=work.width; const q=c.getContext('2d'); q.translate(c.width,0); q.rotate(Math.PI/2); q.drawImage(work,0,0); work=c; fit(); };

    ov.querySelector('#cas').onclick=()=>end(out(0,0,work.width,work.height));
    ov.querySelector('#cno').onclick=()=>end(null); };
  im.src=url; }); }
function shrinkImg(file,max,q,png){ return new Promise((ok,no)=>{ const url=URL.createObjectURL(file); const im=new Image();
  im.onload=()=>{ let w=im.naturalWidth,h=im.naturalHeight; const s=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(w,h)); w=Math.max(1,Math.round(w*s)); h=Math.max(1,Math.round(h*s));
    const c=document.createElement('canvas'); c.width=w; c.height=h; const x=c.getContext('2d'); if(!png){ x.fillStyle='#fff'; x.fillRect(0,0,w,h); } x.drawImage(im,0,0,w,h); URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    const du=png?c.toDataURL('image/png'):c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',q||0.72); ok({du,b64:du.split(',')[1],mime:png?'image/png':'image/jpeg'}); };
  im.onerror=()=>{ URL.revokeObjectURL(url); no(new Error('\u062a\u0639\u0630\u0651\u0631 \u0642\u0631\u0627\u0621\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u0635\u0648\u0631\u0629: '+file.name)); }; im.src=url; }); }

const A4_H=990;
function fitOnePage(p){ const el=p.querySelector('.pf'); if(!el) return; el.style.zoom=''; const w=Math.round((210-18)*96/25.4); p.style.cssText='display:block;position:absolute;left:-9999px;top:0;width:'+w+'px;background:#fff'; const h=el.scrollHeight; p.style.cssText=''; if(h>A4_H) el.style.zoom=String(Math.max(0.5,Math.floor(A4_H/h*1000)/1000)); }
async function printForm(f,o){
  let M; try{ M=await fmMeta(); }catch(e){ return toast(e.message); }
  o=o||fmOrder(f.wo,f); toast('\u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u062a\u062c\u0647\u064a\u0632 \u0627\u0644\u0637\u0628\u0627\u0639\u0629\u2026');
  let sg=f.sg||{sig:'',emp_no:''}; const ak=String(f.data.author||'').trim(); S.sigC=S.sigC||{}; if(f.sg&&ak) S.sigC[ak]=f.sg; if(!f.sg&&ak){ try{ sg=S.sigC[ak]||(S.sigC[ak]=await api('formSig',S.tok,ak)); }catch(e){} }
  let p=$('#prt'); if(!p){ p=document.createElement('div'); p.id='prt'; document.body.appendChild(p); }
  p.innerHTML=fmPrintHtml(f,o,M,sg);
  await Promise.all([...p.querySelectorAll('img')].map(im=>im.complete?1:new Promise(r=>{ im.onload=im.onerror=r; setTimeout(r,12000); })));
  fitOnePage(p); window.print();
}

function fmPrintHtml(f,o,M,sg){
  const d=f.data||{}, e=x=>esc(x==null?'':x), L=M.logos||{}, isMt=(f.data||{}).src==='maint'||!!o.maint, WOL=isMt?'\u0631\u0642\u0645 \u0627\u0644\u0625\u0634\u0639\u0627\u0631 D9':'\u0631\u0642\u0645 \u0627\u0645\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0639\u0645\u0644', woT=isMt?e(String(f.wo||o.wo||'').replace(/^D9:/,'')):e(o.type?o.type+' - ':'')+e(o.wo||woNum(f.wo)), okD=x=>{ x=String(x==null?'':x).slice(0,10); return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(x)&&x>='2000'?x:''; }, dpt=String(d.dept||'').trim()||'\u0627\u0644\u062f\u0645\u0627\u0645', cir=f.office||o.office||'', ph=(f.photos||[]).map(p=>phUrl(p.id,1400));
  const sig=sg.sig?`<img class="sig" src="${sg.sig}">`:'', st=d.stamp!=='0'&&L.stamp;
  const more=ph.length>1?`<div class="more">${ph.slice(1).map(u=>`<img src="${u}">`).join('')}</div>`:'';
  const items=(d.items||[]).slice(); const tot=items.reduce((s,it)=>s+(+(f.kind==='safety'?it.rep:it.qty)||0)*(+it.price||0),0);
  const logos=(r,l)=>`<div class="logos">${r?`<img src="${r}">`:'<span></span>'}${l?`<img src="${l}">`:'<span></span>'}</div>`;
  if(f.kind==='safety'){ while(items.length<4) items.push({});

    return `<div class="pf saf">${logos(L.se,L.pdc)}<div class="page"><table>${C}
     <tr class="hbar"><td colspan="6">\u0646\u0645\u0648\u0630\u062c \u0645\u062d\u0636\u0631 \u0645\u062e\u0627\u0644\u0641\u0629 \u0633\u0644\u0627\u0645\u0629</td></tr>
     <tr><td>\u0631\u0642\u0645 \u0645\u062d\u0636\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u062e\u0627\u0644\u0641\u0629</td><td colspan="5" class="v">${fmNo(f)}</td></tr>
     <tr><td>\u0627\u0644\u0627\u062f\u0627\u0631\u0629</td><td class="v">${e(dpt)}</td><td>\u0627\u0644\u062f\u0627\u0626\u0631\u0629</td><td class="v">${e(cir)}</td><td>\u0627\u0644\u0645\u0643\u062a\u0628</td><td class="v">${e(f.office||o.office)}</td></tr>

        try{ await up(fs[i],name,emp); ok++; }catch(e){ bad.push(name+': '+e.message); } }
      S.meta=null; toast('\u062a\u0645 \u0631\u0641\u0639 '+ok+' \u062a\u0648\u0642\u064a\u0639\u0627\u064b'); draw(); if(bad.length) $('#sgerr').innerHTML=bad.map(esc).join('<br>'); };

  draw();
}

function vJourney(){
  let J=(S.B.journey||[]).map(x=>Object.assign({},x)); const names=S.B.people.map(p=>p.name);
  const nrm=x=>String(x==null?'':x).replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim(), OFF=S.B.lists.office||[], OK=OFF.map((x,n)=>'o'+(n+1)), blank=()=>{ const r={kind:'\u0645\u0631\u062d\u0644\u0629',code:'',name:'',pct:'',big:'\u064a\u062f\u0648\u064a\u0629'}; OK.forEach(k=>r[k]=''); return r; };
  const PJ=S.B.people.filter(p=>jobSplit(p.job).length).map(p=>({name:p.name,jobs:jobSplit(p.job),offs:String(p.offices||'').split(/[,\u060c]/).map(nrm).filter(Boolean)}));
  const jobs=ORDER_ROLES.concat([...new Set(jobsAll().concat(...PJ.map(p=>p.jobs)))].filter(j=>!ORDER_ROLES.includes(j)).sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'ar'))); const isJob=v=>jobs.includes(nrm(v));
  const jOwn=(v,off)=>{ const c=PJ.filter(p=>p.jobs.includes(nrm(v))&&p.offs.includes(off)).sort((a,b)=>a.offs.length-b.offs.length)[0]; return c?c.name:''; };

function buildReport(maint,month,share){ const RP_SHARE=(+share>0?+share:60)/100;
  const end=month?month+'-31':'9999', upTo=o=>!o.assign_date||String(o.assign_date).slice(0,10)<=end; const ORD=S.orders.concat(S.archO||[]);
  const bad=[]; const sites=rpSites().map(([en,ar])=>{ const all=ORD.filter(o=>{ if(o.office!==ar||o.cost===''||o.cost==null||isNaN(+o.cost)||!upTo(o)) return false; if(+o.cost<=0){ bad.push(o.wo); return false; } return true; }); const small=all.filter(o=>+o.cost<=50000), big=all.filter(o=>+o.cost>50000);
    const c=small.reduce((s,o)=>s+(+o.cost),0), p=small.length?small.reduce((s,o)=>s+jPct(o),0)/small.length/100:0;
    const rows=[{label:'Metering Unit ('+small.length+')',pdc:r2(c),aji:r2(c*RP_SHARE),pct:Math.round(p*10000)/10000,sar:r2(c*RP_SHARE*p)}].concat(big.map(o=>{ const pc=bigPct(o)/100;
      return {wo:o.wo,key:o.key,pdc:r2(+o.cost),aji:r2(+o.cost*RP_SHARE),pct:pc,sar:r2(+o.cost*RP_SHARE*pc),contractor:o.contractor,office:o.office,manual:bigManual(o)?1:0}; }));
    const t=k=>r2(rows.reduce((s,x)=>s+x[k],0)); return {en,ar,rows,tot:{pdc:t('pdc'),aji:t('aji'),sar:t('sar')}}; });
  const mp=(+maint.pct||0)/100, m={n:maint.n,aji:r2(maint.sum),pct:mp,sar:r2(maint.sum*mp)};
  const g=k=>r2(sites.reduce((x,y)=>x+y.tot[k],0)+(k==='pdc'?0:m[k])), aji=g('aji'), sar=g('sar');
  return {sites,maint:m,cum:{pdc:g('pdc'),aji,sar,pct:aji?Math.round(sar/aji*10000)/10000:0},month:month||'',made:S.B.today,share:Math.round(RP_SHARE*10000)/100,bad};
}
const RPH=['Site','Scheme Number<br>(Meter Units+Projects)','Total Project Cost -PDC','Total Project Cost -AJI','Project Progress',' Progress (SAR)'];
const m2=x=>(+x||0)?(+x).toLocaleString('en',{minimumFractionDigits:2,maximumFractionDigits:2}):'-';
function reportHtml(rep,edit){
  const pc=x=>(+x||0)?Math.round(+x*100)+'%':'-';
  const body=rep.sites.map(s=>s.rows.map((x,i)=>`<tr class="${i?'':'mu'}">${i?'':`<td class="site" rowspan="${s.rows.length}">${esc(s.en)}</td>`}<td>${x.label?esc(x.label):esc(x.wo)}</td><td>${m2(x.pdc)}</td><td>${m2(x.aji)}</td>
      <td>${edit&&x.manual?`<input data-rp="${esc(x.key)}" value="${Math.round(x.pct*100)}" inputmode="numeric" style="width:54px;text-align:center" title="\u0646\u0633\u0628\u0629 \u064a\u062f\u0648\u064a\u0629"> %`:pc(x.pct)}</td><td>${m2(x.sar)}</td><td class="x">${esc(x.contractor||'')}</td><td class="x">${esc(x.office||'')}</td></tr>`).join('')
      +`<tr class="tot"><td>Total</td><td></td><td>${m2(s.tot.pdc)}</td><td>${m2(s.tot.aji)}</td><td></td><td>${m2(s.tot.sar)}</td><td class="x"></td><td class="x"></td></tr>`).join('');
  const m=rep.maint;
  return `<table class="rpt"><thead><tr>${RPH.map(h=>`<th>${h}</th>`).join('')}<th class="x"></th><th class="x"></th></tr></thead><tbody>${body}
    <tr><td class="site">Maintenance &amp; Emergency</td><td>\u0627\u0644\u0639\u062f\u062f ${m.n}</td><td></td><td>${m2(m.aji)}</td><td>${pc(m.pct)}</td><td>${m2(m.sar)}</td><td class="x"></td><td class="x"></td></tr>
    <tr class="tot"><td>Total</td><td></td><td>${m2(0)}</td><td>${m2(m.aji)}</td><td></td><td>${m2(m.sar)}</td><td class="x"></td><td class="x"></td></tr>
    ${rep.cum?`<tr class="cum"><td colspan="2">Cumulative${rep.month?' to end of '+esc(rep.month):''}</td><td>${m2(rep.cum.pdc)}</td><td>${m2(rep.cum.aji)}</td><td>${(rep.cum.pct*100).toFixed(1)}%</td><td>${m2(rep.cum.sar)}</td><td class="x"></td><td class="x"></td></tr>`:''}</tbody></table>`;
}

const CRCT=(()=>{ const t=new Uint32Array(256); for(let n=0;n<256;n++){ let c=n; for(let k=0;k<8;k++) c=c&1?0xEDB88320^(c>>>1):c>>>1; t[n]=c>>>0; } return t; })();
const crc32=b=>{ let c=0xFFFFFFFF; for(let i=0;i<b.length;i++) c=CRCT[(c^b[i])&255]^(c>>>8); return (c^0xFFFFFFFF)>>>0; };
function zipStore(files,type){ const enc=new TextEncoder(), parts=[], cen=[]; let off=0;
  files.forEach(([name,text])=>{ const nb=enc.encode(name), data=enc.encode(text), crc=crc32(data);
    const lh=new DataView(new ArrayBuffer(30)); lh.setUint32(0,0x04034b50,true); lh.setUint16(4,20,true); lh.setUint16(6,0x0800,true); lh.setUint16(12,0x21,true); lh.setUint32(14,crc,true); lh.setUint32(18,data.length,true); lh.setUint32(22,data.length,true); lh.setUint16(26,nb.length,true);
    const ch=new DataView(new ArrayBuffer(46)); ch.setUint32(0,0x02014b50,true); ch.setUint16(4,20,true); ch.setUint16(6,20,true); ch.setUint16(8,0x0800,true); ch.setUint16(14,0x21,true); ch.setUint32(16,crc,true); ch.setUint32(20,data.length,true); ch.setUint32(24,data.length,true); ch.setUint16(28,nb.length,true); ch.setUint32(42,off,true);
    parts.push(lh.buffer,nb,data); cen.push(ch.buffer,nb); off+=30+nb.length+data.length; });
  const cs=cen.reduce((x,y)=>x+y.byteLength,0), e=new DataView(new ArrayBuffer(22)); e.setUint32(0,0x06054b50,true); e.setUint16(8,files.length,true); e.setUint16(10,files.length,true); e.setUint32(12,cs,true); e.setUint32(16,off,true);
  return new Blob(parts.concat(cen,[e.buffer]),{type}); }
function reportXlsx(rep,title){
  const X=t=>String(t).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
  const O='http:\/\/schemas.openxmlformats.org\/', NS=O+'spreadsheetml\/2006\/main', RL=O+'officeDocument\/2006\/relationships', PK=O+'package\/2006\/', HEAD='<'+'?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?'+'>';
  const rows=[], merges=[], tots=[]; let n=0;
  const cell=(col,v,st)=>{ const r=col+n; if(v==null||v==='') return `<c r="${r}" s="${st}"/>`; if(typeof v==='object') return `<c r="${r}" s="${st}"><f>${X(v.f)}</f><v>${v.v}</v></c>`;

const XU_D='<'+'?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?'+'>\n';
const xuCol=n=>{ let s=''; n++; while(n>0){ const m=(n-1)%26; s=String.fromCharCode(65+m)+s; n=Math.floor((n-1)/26); } return s; };
const xuX=s=>String(s==null?'':s).replace(/[\u0000-\u0008\u000b\u000c\u000e-\u001f\ufffe\uffff]/g,'').replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
const xuSer=d=>{ const m=/^(\d{4})[-\/](\d{1,2})[-\/](\d{1,2})$/.exec(String(d==null?'':d).trim()); if(!m||+m[1]<1990||+m[1]>2100||+m[2]<1||+m[2]>12||+m[3]<1||+m[3]>31) return null; return Math.round(Date.UTC(+m[1],+m[2]-1,+m[3])/864e5)+25569; };
const xuQ=n=>"'"+String(n).replace(/'/g,"''")+"'";
const xuBin=b64=>{ const s=atob(String(b64||'').replace(/^data:[^,]*,/,'')); const u=new Uint8Array(s.length); for(let i=0;i<s.length;i++) u[i]=s.charCodeAt(i); return u; };
const xuNum=x=>{ if(typeof x==='number') return isFinite(x)?x:null; const s=String(x==null?'':x).replace(/,/g,'').trim(); return /^-?\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(s)&&s.replace(/[-.]/g,'').length<=15?+s:null; };
function xuStyles(){
  const fonts=['<font><sz val="11"/><color theme="1"/><name val="Calibri"/><family val="2"/><scheme val="minor"/></font>'], fills=['<fill><patternFill patternType="none"/></fill>','<fill><patternFill patternType="gray125"/></fill>'],
    borders=['<border><left/><right/><top/><bottom/><diagonal/></border>'], nfs=[], xfs=['<xf numFmtId="0" fontId="0" fillId="0" borderId="0" xfId="0"/>'], dxfs=[], cache=new Map(), NF={'General':0,'0':1,'0.00':2,'#,##0':3,'#,##0.00':4};
  const idx=(arr,x)=>{ let i=arr.indexOf(x); if(i<0){ arr.push(x); i=arr.length-1; } return i; };
  const side=(t,c,st)=>'<'+t+' style="'+st+'"><color rgb="FF'+c+'"/></'+t+'>';
  const id=sp=>{ if(!sp) return 0; let r=cache.get(sp); if(r!==undefined) return r; const k=JSON.stringify(sp); r=cache.get(k);
    if(r===undefined){
      const font=(sp.b||sp.sz||sp.fc||sp.u)?idx(fonts,'<font>'+(sp.b?'<b/>':'')+(sp.u?'<u/>':'')+'<sz val="'+(sp.sz||11)+'"/>'+(sp.fc?'<color rgb="FF'+sp.fc+'"/>':'<color theme="1"/>')+'<name val="Calibri"/><family val="2"/><scheme val="minor"/></font>'):0;
      const fill=sp.fill?idx(fills,'<fill><patternFill patternType="solid"><fgColor rgb="FF'+sp.fill+'"/><bgColor indexed="64"/></patternFill></fill>'):0;
      const border=sp.bd?idx(borders,'<border>'+side('left','D3D1C7','thin')+side('right','D3D1C7','thin')+side('top','D3D1C7','thin')+(sp.bb?side('bottom',sp.bb,'medium'):side('bottom','D3D1C7','thin'))+'<diagonal/></border>'):0;
      let nf=0; if(sp.nf){ nf=NF[sp.nf]; if(nf===undefined) nf=164+idx(nfs,sp.nf); }
      const al=(sp.al||sp.wrap||sp.va)?'<alignment'+(sp.al?' horizontal="'+sp.al+'"':'')+(sp.va?' vertical="'+sp.va+'"':'')+(sp.wrap?' wrapText="1"':'')+'/>':'';
      const pr=sp.ul?'<protection locked="0"/>':'';
      xfs.push('<xf numFmtId="'+nf+'" fontId="'+font+'" fillId="'+fill+'" borderId="'+border+'" xfId="0"'+(nf?' applyNumberFormat="1"':'')+(font?' applyFont="1"':'')+(fill?' applyFill="1"':'')+(border?' applyBorder="1"':'')+(pr?' applyProtection="1"':'')+((al||pr)?' applyAlignment="1">'+al+pr+'</xf>':'/>'));
      r=xfs.length-1; cache.set(k,r); }
    cache.set(sp,r); return r; };
  const dxf=(fc,bg)=>idx(dxfs,'<dxf><font><color rgb="FF'+fc+'"/></font><fill><patternFill><bgColor rgb="FF'+bg+'"/></patternFill></fill></dxf>');
  const xml=()=>XU_D+'<styleSheet xmlns="'+XU_M+'">'+(nfs.length?'<numFmts count="'+nfs.length+'">'+nfs.map((f,i)=>'<numFmt numFmtId="'+(164+i)+'" formatCode="'+xuX(f)+'"/>').join('')+'</numFmts>':'')
    +'<fonts count="'+fonts.length+'">'+fonts.join('')+'</fonts><fills count="'+fills.length+'">'+fills.join('')+'</fills><borders count="'+borders.length+'">'+borders.join('')+'</borders>'
    +'<cellStyleXfs count="1"><xf numFmtId="0" fontId="0" fillId="0" borderId="0"/></cellStyleXfs><cellXfs count="'+xfs.length+'">'+xfs.join('')+'</cellXfs><cellStyles count="1"><cellStyle name="Normal" xfId="0" builtinId="0"/></cellStyles>'
    +(dxfs.length?'<dxfs count="'+dxfs.length+'">'+dxfs.join('')+'</dxfs>':'<dxfs count="0"/>')+'<tableStyles count="0" defaultTableStyle="TableStyleMedium9" defaultPivotStyle="PivotStyleLight16"/></styleSheet>';
  return {id,dxf,xml};
}
function xuSheetXml(sh,st,first,drawRel){
  let maxc=0, dyn=0; const body=[];
  sh.rows.forEach((row,r)=>{ if(!row) return; let s=''; if(row.length>maxc) maxc=row.length;
    for(let c=0;c<row.length;c++){ const x=row[c]; if(x==null) continue; const ref=xuCol(c)+(r+1), sid=st.id(x.st), sa=sid?' s="'+sid+'"':'', v=(typeof x.v==='number'&&!isFinite(x.v))?'':x.v;
      if(x.f!=null){ const isS=typeof v==='string'; if(x.dyn) dyn++;
        s+='<c r="'+ref+'"'+sa+(isS?' t="str"':'')+(x.dyn?' cm="1"':'')+'>'+(x.dyn?'<f t="array" ref="'+(x.ref||ref)+'">':'<f>')+xuX(x.f)+'</f>'+(v==null||v===''?(isS?'<v></v>':''):'<v>'+xuX(v)+'</v>')+'</c>'; }
      else if(v==null||v==='') s+=sid?'<c r="'+ref+'"'+sa+'/>':'';
      else if(typeof v==='number') s+='<c r="'+ref+'"'+sa+'><v>'+v+'</v></c>';
      else if(x.rich&&x.rich.length) s+='<c r="'+ref+'"'+sa+' t="inlineStr"><is>'+x.rich.map(q=>'<r>'+(q.c?'<rPr><b/><color rgb="FF'+q.c+'"/></rPr>':'')+'<t xml:space="preserve">'+xuX(q.t)+'</t></r>').join('')+'</is></c>';
      else s+='<c r="'+ref+'"'+sa+' t="inlineStr"><is><t xml:space="preserve">'+xuX(v)+'</t></is></c>'; }
    const ht=sh.hts&&sh.hts[r]; if(s||ht) body.push('<row r="'+(r+1)+'"'+(ht?' ht="'+ht+'" customHeight="1"':'')+'>'+s+'</row>'); });
  const nr=sh.rows.length, last=xuCol(Math.max(maxc,1)-1)+Math.max(nr,1), fz=sh.freeze||[0,0];
  let pane='';
  if(fz[0]&&fz[1]) pane='<pane xSplit="'+fz[0]+'" ySplit="'+fz[1]+'" topLeftCell="'+xuCol(fz[0])+(fz[1]+1)+'" activePane="bottomRight" state="frozen"/><selection pane="topRight" activeCell="'+xuCol(fz[0])+'1" sqref="'+xuCol(fz[0])+'1"/><selection pane="bottomLeft" activeCell="A'+(fz[1]+1)+'" sqref="A'+(fz[1]+1)+'"/><selection pane="bottomRight"/>';
  else if(fz[1]) pane='<pane ySplit="'+fz[1]+'" topLeftCell="A'+(fz[1]+1)+'" activePane="bottomLeft" state="frozen"/><selection pane="bottomLeft"/>';
  const cols=(sh.widths||[]).length?'<cols>'+sh.widths.map((w,i)=>'<col min="'+(i+1)+'" max="'+(i+1)+'" width="'+w+'" customWidth="1"/>').join('')+'</cols>':'';
  let pr=0; const cf=(sh.cf||[]).map(x=>'<conditionalFormatting sqref="'+x.sqref+'"><cfRule type="cellIs" dxfId="'+x.dxf+'" priority="'+(++pr)+'" operator="'+x.op+'"><formula>'+xuX(x.val)+'</formula></cfRule></conditionalFormatting>').join('');
  const dv=(sh.valid||[]).length?'<dataValidations count="'+sh.valid.length+'">'+sh.valid.map(x=>'<dataValidation type="list" allowBlank="1" showInputMessage="1"'+(x.free?'':' showErrorMessage="1"')+' sqref="'+x.sqref+'"><formula1>'+xuX(x.ref)+'</formula1></dataValidation>').join('')+'</dataValidations>':'';
  const mg=(sh.merges||[]).length?'<mergeCells count="'+sh.merges.length+'">'+sh.merges.map(m=>'<mergeCell ref="'+m+'"/>').join('')+'</mergeCells>':'';
  const spr=(sh.color||sh.print)?'<sheetPr>'+(sh.color?'<tabColor rgb="FF'+sh.color+'"/>':'')+(sh.print?'<pageSetUpPr fitToPage="1"/>':'')+'</sheetPr>':'';
  const xml=XU_D+'<worksheet xmlns="'+XU_M+'" xmlns:r="'+XU_R+'">'+spr+'<dimension ref="A1:'+last+'"/>'
    +'<sheetViews><sheetView rightToLeft="1"'+(first?' tabSelected="1"':'')+(sh.nogrid?' showGridLines="0"':'')+' workbookViewId="0">'+pane+'</sheetView></sheetViews><sheetFormatPr defaultRowHeight="15"/>'+cols+'<sheetData>'+body.join('')+'</sheetData>'
    +(sh.protect?'<sheetProtection sheet="1" objects="1" scenarios="1" autoFilter="0" sort="0" formatColumns="0" formatRows="0" insertRows="0" deleteRows="0" formatCells="0"/>':'')+(sh.filter?'<autoFilter ref="'+sh.filter+'"/>':'')+mg+cf+dv+(sh.print?'<printOptions horizontalCentered="1"/>':'')+'<pageMargins left="0.4" right="0.4" top="0.5" bottom="0.5" header="0.3" footer="0.3"/>'+(sh.print?'<pageSetup paperSize="9" orientation="portrait" fitToWidth="1" fitToHeight="1"/>':'')+'<ignoredErrors><ignoredError sqref="A1:XFD1048576" numberStoredAsText="1"/></ignoredErrors>'+(drawRel?'<drawing r:id="'+drawRel+'"/>':'')+'</worksheet>';
  return {xml,dyn};
}
function xuChartXml(ch,n){
  const cache=(vals,num)=>(num?'<c:numCache><c:formatCode>General</c:formatCode>':'<c:strCache>')+'<c:ptCount val="'+vals.length+'"/>'+vals.map((v,i)=>'<c:pt idx="'+i+'"><c:v>'+xuX(num?(+v||0):v)+'</c:v></c:pt>').join('')+(num?'</c:numCache>':'</c:strCache>');
  const cat='<c:cat><c:strRef><c:f>'+xuX(ch.sheet+'!'+ch.catRef)+'</c:f>'+cache(ch.cats,0)+'</c:strRef></c:cat>';
  const ser=ch.series.map((s,i)=>'<c:ser><c:idx val="'+i+'"/><c:order val="'+i+'"/><c:tx><c:v>'+xuX(s.name)+'</c:v></c:tx>'
    +(ch.kind==='pie'?ch.cats.map((x,j)=>'<c:dPt><c:idx val="'+j+'"/><c:spPr><a:solidFill><a:srgbClr val="'+ch.colors[j%ch.colors.length]+'"/></a:solidFill></c:spPr></c:dPt>').join('')+'<c:dLbls><c:showVal val="1"/><c:showPercent val="1"/><c:separator>\n</c:separator><c:showLeaderLines val="1"/></c:dLbls>'
      :'<c:spPr><a:solidFill><a:srgbClr val="'+s.color+'"/></a:solidFill></c:spPr><c:dLbls><c:showVal val="1"/></c:dLbls>')
    +cat+'<c:val><c:numRef><c:f>'+xuX(ch.sheet+'!'+s.ref)+'</c:f>'+cache(s.vals,1)+'</c:numRef></c:val></c:ser>').join('');
  const a1=5000+n*10+1, a2=5000+n*10+2, hz=ch.kind==='bar';
  const plot=ch.kind==='pie'?'<c:pieChart><c:varyColors val="1"/>'+ser+'<c:firstSliceAng val="0"/></c:pieChart>'
    :'<c:barChart><c:barDir val="'+(hz?'bar':'col')+'"/><c:grouping val="clustered"/>'+ser+'<c:gapWidth val="60"/><c:axId val="'+a1+'"/><c:axId val="'+a2+'"/></c:barChart>'
     +'<c:catAx><c:axId val="'+a1+'"/><c:scaling><c:orientation val="'+(hz?'maxMin':'minMax')+'"/></c:scaling><c:axPos val="'+(hz?'l':'b')+'"/><c:tickLblPos val="nextTo"/><c:crossAx val="'+a2+'"/><c:crosses val="autoZero"/><c:auto val="1"/><c:lblAlgn val="ctr"/><c:lblOffset val="100"/></c:catAx>'
     +'<c:valAx><c:axId val="'+a2+'"/><c:scaling><c:orientation val="minMax"/></c:scaling><c:axPos val="'+(hz?'t':'l')+'"/><c:majorGridlines/><c:numFmt formatCode="General" sourceLinked="1"/><c:tickLblPos val="nextTo"/><c:crossAx val="'+a1+'"/><c:crosses val="autoZero"/><c:crossBetween val="between"/></c:valAx>';
  const legend=(ch.kind==='pie'||ch.series.length>1)?'<c:legend><c:legendPos val="'+(ch.kind==='pie'?'r':'b')+'"/><c:layout/><c:txPr><a:bodyPr/><a:lstStyle/><a:p><a:pPr rtl="0"><a:defRPr/></a:pPr><a:endParaRPr lang="en-US"/></a:p></c:txPr></c:legend>':'';
  return XU_D+'<c:chartSpace xmlns:c="'+XU_C+'" xmlns:a="'+XU_A+'" xmlns:r="'+XU_R+'"><c:lang val="en-US"/><c:chart><c:title><c:tx><c:rich><a:bodyPr/><a:lstStyle/><a:p><a:pPr><a:defRPr sz="1200" b="1" baseline="0"/></a:pPr><a:r><a:rPr lang="en-US" sz="1200" b="1" baseline="0"/><a:t>'+xuX(ch.title)+'</a:t></a:r></a:p></c:rich></c:tx><c:layout/></c:title>'
    +'<c:plotArea><c:layout/>'+plot+'</c:plotArea>'+legend+'<c:plotVisOnly val="1"/></c:chart><c:printSettings><c:headerFooter/><c:pageMargins b="0.75" l="0.7" r="0.7" t="0.75" header="0.3" footer="0.3"/><c:pageSetup/></c:printSettings></c:chartSpace>';
}
function xuParts(model){
  const st=xuStyles(), parts={}, CT=[], wbRels=[], sheetsXml=[], names=[]; let nDyn=0, nCh=0, nDr=0; const rel=(id,type,target)=>'<Relationship Id="'+id+'" Type="'+XU_R+'/'+type+'" Target="'+target+'"/>';
  model.sheets.forEach(sh=>{ (sh.pre||[]).forEach(fn=>fn(st)); });
  let nImg=0; const media={};
  model.sheets.forEach((sh,i)=>{ const n=i+1; let drawRel='';
    if((sh.charts&&sh.charts.length)||(sh.images&&sh.images.length)){ nDr++; drawRel='rId1'; const dr=[], drr=[]; sh.charts=sh.charts||[];
      sh.charts.forEach((ch,j)=>{ nCh++; parts['xl/charts/chart'+nCh+'.xml']=xuChartXml(ch,nCh); CT.push('<Override PartName="/xl/charts/chart'+nCh+'.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.drawingml.chart+xml"/>');
        drr.push(rel('rId'+(j+1),'chart','../charts/chart'+nCh+'.xml')); const p=ch.pos;
        dr.push('<xdr:twoCellAnchor><xdr:from><xdr:col>'+p.c+'</xdr:col><xdr:colOff>0</xdr:colOff><xdr:row>'+p.r+'</xdr:row><xdr:rowOff>0</xdr:rowOff></xdr:from><xdr:to><xdr:col>'+(p.c+p.w)+'</xdr:col><xdr:colOff>0</xdr:colOff><xdr:row>'+(p.r+p.h)+'</xdr:row><xdr:rowOff>0</xdr:rowOff></xdr:to>'
          +'<xdr:graphicFrame macro=""><xdr:nvGraphicFramePr><xdr:cNvPr id="'+(j+2)+'" name="Chart '+(j+1)+'"/><xdr:cNvGraphicFramePr/></xdr:nvGraphicFramePr><xdr:xfrm><a:off x="0" y="0"/><a:ext cx="0" cy="0"/></xdr:xfrm><a:graphic><a:graphicData uri="'+XU_C+'"><c:chart xmlns:c="'+XU_C+'" xmlns:r="'+XU_R+'" r:id="rId'+(j+1)+'"/></a:graphicData></a:graphic></xdr:graphicFrame><xdr:clientData/></xdr:twoCellAnchor>'); });
      (sh.images||[]).forEach((im,j)=>{ nImg++; const ext=im.ext||'png', key=im.key||('i'+nImg); let fname=media[key]; if(!fname){ fname='image'+nImg+'.'+ext; media[key]=fname; parts['xl/media/'+fname]=xuBin(im.b64); }
        const rid='rId'+(sh.charts.length+j+1); drr.push(rel(rid,'image','../media/'+fname)); const p=im.from, q=im.to;
        dr.push('<xdr:twoCellAnchor editAs="oneCell"><xdr:from><xdr:col>'+p.c+'</xdr:col><xdr:colOff>'+(p.co||0)+'</xdr:colOff><xdr:row>'+p.r+'</xdr:row><xdr:rowOff>'+(p.ro||0)+'</xdr:rowOff></xdr:from><xdr:to><xdr:col>'+q.c+'</xdr:col><xdr:colOff>'+(q.co||0)+'</xdr:colOff><xdr:row>'+q.r+'</xdr:row><xdr:rowOff>'+(q.ro||0)+'</xdr:rowOff></xdr:to>'
          +'<xdr:pic><xdr:nvPicPr><xdr:cNvPr id="'+(sh.charts.length+j+2)+'" name="Picture '+(j+1)+'"/><xdr:cNvPicPr><a:picLocks noChangeAspect="1"/></xdr:cNvPicPr></xdr:nvPicPr><xdr:blipFill><a:blip xmlns:r="'+XU_R+'" r:embed="'+rid+'"/><a:stretch><a:fillRect/></a:stretch></xdr:blipFill><xdr:spPr><a:xfrm><a:off x="0" y="0"/><a:ext cx="0" cy="0"/></a:xfrm><a:prstGeom prst="rect"><a:avLst/></a:prstGeom></xdr:spPr></xdr:pic><xdr:clientData/></xdr:twoCellAnchor>'); });
      parts['xl/drawings/drawing'+nDr+'.xml']=XU_D+'<xdr:wsDr xmlns:xdr="'+xuU('schemas.openxmlformats.org/drawingml/2006/spreadsheetDrawing')+'" xmlns:a="'+XU_A+'">'+dr.join('')+'</xdr:wsDr>';
      parts['xl/drawings/_rels/drawing'+nDr+'.xml.rels']=XU_D+'<Relationships xmlns="'+XU_P+'">'+drr.join('')+'</Relationships>';
      parts['xl/worksheets/_rels/sheet'+n+'.xml.rels']=XU_D+'<Relationships xmlns="'+XU_P+'">'+rel('rId1','drawing','../drawings/drawing'+nDr+'.xml')+'</Relationships>';
      CT.push('<Override PartName="/xl/drawings/drawing'+nDr+'.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.drawing+xml"/>'); }
    const r=xuSheetXml(sh,st,i===0,drawRel); nDyn+=r.dyn; parts['xl/worksheets/sheet'+n+'.xml']=r.xml;
    CT.push('<Override PartName="/xl/worksheets/sheet'+n+'.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.worksheet+xml"/>');
    wbRels.push(rel('rId'+n,'worksheet','worksheets/sheet'+n+'.xml')); sheetsXml.push('<sheet name="'+xuX(sh.name)+'" sheetId="'+n+'"'+(sh.hidden?' state="hidden"':'')+' r:id="rId'+n+'"/>');
    if(sh.filter) names.push('<definedName name="_xlnm._FilterDatabase" localSheetId="'+i+'" hidden="1">'+xuX(xuQ(sh.name)+'!'+sh.filter.replace(/([A-Z]+)(\d+)/g,'$$$1$$$2'))+'</definedName>'); });
  let k=model.sheets.length; wbRels.push(rel('rId'+(++k),'theme','theme/theme1.xml')); wbRels.push(rel('rId'+(++k),'styles','styles.xml')); if(nDyn) wbRels.push(rel('rId'+(++k),'sheetMetadata','metadata.xml'));
  parts['xl/workbook.xml']=XU_D+'<workbook xmlns="'+XU_M+'" xmlns:r="'+XU_R+'"><fileVersion appName="xl" lastEdited="4" lowestEdited="4" rupBuild="4505"/><workbookPr defaultThemeVersion="124226"/><bookViews><workbookView xWindow="240" yWindow="15" windowWidth="16095" windowHeight="9660"/></bookViews><sheets>'+sheetsXml.join('')+'</sheets>'+(names.length?'<definedNames>'+names.join('')+'</definedNames>':'')+'<calcPr calcId="124519" fullCalcOnLoad="1"/></workbook>';
  parts['xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels']=XU_D+'<Relationships xmlns="'+XU_P+'">'+wbRels.join('')+'</Relationships>';
  parts['xl/styles.xml']=st.xml(); parts['xl/theme/theme1.xml']=XU_D+XU_THEME;
  if(nDyn) parts['xl/metadata.xml']=XU_D+'<metadata xmlns="'+XU_M+'" xmlns:xda="'+xuU('schemas.microsoft.com/office/spreadsheetml/2017/dynamicarray')+'"><metadataTypes count="1"><metadataType name="XLDAPR" minSupportedVersion="120000" copy="1" pasteAll="1" pasteValues="1" merge="1" splitFirst="1" rowColShift="1" clearFormats="1" clearComments="1" assign="1" coerce="1" cellMeta="1"/></metadataTypes><futureMetadata name="XLDAPR" count="1"><bk><extLst><ext uri="{bdbb8cdc-fa1e-496e-a857-3c3f30c029c3}"><xda:dynamicArrayProperties fDynamic="1" fCollapsed="0"/></ext></extLst></bk></futureMetadata><cellMetadata count="1"><bk><rc t="1" v="0"/></bk></cellMetadata></metadata>';
  const now=new Date().toISOString().slice(0,19)+'Z';
  parts['docProps/app.xml']=XU_D+'<Properties xmlns="'+xuU('schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/extended-properties')+'" xmlns:vt="'+xuU('schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/docPropsVTypes')+'"><Application>Microsoft Excel</Application><DocSecurity>0</DocSecurity><ScaleCrop>false</ScaleCrop><Company></Company><LinksUpToDate>false</LinksUpToDate><SharedDoc>false</SharedDoc><HyperlinksChanged>false</HyperlinksChanged><AppVersion>12.0000</AppVersion></Properties>';
  parts['docProps/core.xml']=XU_D+'<cp:coreProperties xmlns:cp="'+xuU('schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/metadata/core-properties')+'" xmlns:dc="'+xuU('purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/')+'" xmlns:dcterms="'+xuU('purl.org/dc/terms/')+'" xmlns:dcmitype="'+xuU('purl.org/dc/dcmitype/')+'" xmlns:xsi="'+xuU('www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance')+'"><dc:creator>'+xuX(model.author||'')+'</dc:creator><cp:lastModifiedBy>'+xuX(model.author||'')+'</cp:lastModifiedBy><dcterms:created xsi:type="dcterms:W3CDTF">'+now+'</dcterms:created><dcterms:modified xsi:type="dcterms:W3CDTF">'+now+'</dcterms:modified></cp:coreProperties>';
  parts['_rels/.rels']=XU_D+'<Relationships xmlns="'+XU_P+'">'+rel('rId1','officeDocument','xl/workbook.xml')+'<Relationship Id="rId2" Type="'+xuU('schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships/metadata/core-properties')+'" Target="docProps/core.xml"/>'+rel('rId3','extended-properties','docProps/app.xml')+'</Relationships>';

}
async function xuZip(parts){
  const T=new Uint32Array(256); for(let n=0;n<256;n++){ let c=n; for(let k=0;k<8;k++) c=(c&1)?(0xEDB88320^(c>>>1)):(c>>>1); T[n]=c>>>0; }
  const crc=b=>{ let c=0xFFFFFFFF; for(let i=0;i<b.length;i++) c=T[(c^b[i])&0xFF]^(c>>>8); return (c^0xFFFFFFFF)>>>0; };
  const enc=new TextEncoder(), order=Object.keys(parts).sort((a,b)=>(a==='[Content_Types].xml'?-1:b==='[Content_Types].xml'?1:0)), chunks=[], cd=[]; let off=0;
  const can=typeof CompressionStream==='function';
  const u16=(a,o,v)=>{ a[o]=v&255; a[o+1]=(v>>>8)&255; }, u32=(a,o,v)=>{ a[o]=v&255; a[o+1]=(v>>>8)&255; a[o+2]=(v>>>16)&255; a[o+3]=(v>>>24)&255; };
  for(const name of order){ const raw=typeof parts[name]==='string'?enc.encode(parts[name]):parts[name], nm=enc.encode(name), cr=crc(raw); let data=raw, method=0;
    if(can){ try{ const z=new Uint8Array(await new Response(new Blob([raw]).stream().pipeThrough(new CompressionStream('deflate-raw'))).arrayBuffer()); if(z.length<raw.length){ data=z; method=8; } }catch(e){} }
    const h=new Uint8Array(30+nm.length); u32(h,0,0x04034b50); u16(h,4,20); u16(h,6,0x0800); u16(h,8,method); u16(h,10,0); u16(h,12,0x21); u32(h,14,cr); u32(h,18,data.length); u32(h,22,raw.length); u16(h,26,nm.length); u16(h,28,0); h.set(nm,30);
    const c=new Uint8Array(46+nm.length); u32(c,0,0x02014b50); u16(c,4,20); u16(c,6,20); u16(c,8,0x0800); u16(c,10,method); u16(c,12,0); u16(c,14,0x21); u32(c,16,cr); u32(c,20,data.length); u32(c,24,raw.length); u16(c,28,nm.length); u32(c,42,off); c.set(nm,46);
    chunks.push(h,data); cd.push(c); off+=h.length+data.length; }
  const cdSize=cd.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.length,0), e=new Uint8Array(22); u32(e,0,0x06054b50); u16(e,8,cd.length); u16(e,10,cd.length); u32(e,12,cdSize); u32(e,16,off);
  return new Blob(chunks.concat(cd,[e]),{type:'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet'});
}

async function xuGather(say){
  const D={orders:S.orders}, obj=(H,x)=>{ const o={}; H.forEach((k,i)=>o[k]=x[i]); return o; }, miss=[];
  if(tabOn('forms')){ say('\u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u062a\u062d\u0645\u064a\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u062e\u0627\u0644\u0641\u0627\u062a \u0648\u0627\u0644\u0645\u062d\u0627\u0636\u0631\u2026'); try{ const R=S.fxList||(S.fxList=await api('exportForms',S.tok)); D.forms=R.forms; D.logos=R.logos||{}; }catch(e){ miss.push('\u0627\u0644\u0645\u062e\u0627\u0644\u0641\u0627\u062a \u0648\u0627\u0644\u0645\u062d\u0627\u0636\u0631'); } }
  if(tabOn('daily')){ say('\u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u062a\u062d\u0645\u064a\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u062c\u062f\u0648\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u064a\u0648\u0645\u064a\u2026'); try{ const r=await api('listDaily',S.tok,'2000-01-01','2099-12-31'); D.daily=r.rows.map(x=>obj(r.headers,x)); }catch(e){ miss.push('\u0627\u0644\u062c\u062f\u0648\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u064a\u0648\u0645\u064a'); } }
  if(tabOn('maint')){ say('\u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u062a\u062d\u0645\u064a\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u0635\u064a\u0627\u0646\u0629 \u0648\u0627\u0644\u0637\u0648\u0627\u0631\u0626\u2026'); try{ const R=S.mt||(S.mt=await api('listMaint',S.tok)); D.maint=R.L||(R.L=R.rows.map(x=>obj(R.headers,x))); }catch(e){ miss.push('\u0627\u0644\u0635\u064a\u0627\u0646\u0629 \u0648\u0627\u0644\u0637\u0648\u0627\u0631\u0626'); } }
  if(tabOn('report')){ say('\u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u062a\u062d\u0645\u064a\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u062a\u0642\u0631\u064a\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0634\u0647\u0631\u064a\u2026'); try{ D.rp=await api('reportMeta',S.tok,''); }catch(e){ D.rp=null; } }
  if(tabOn('permits')){ say('\u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u062a\u062d\u0645\u064a\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u062a\u0635\u0627\u0631\u064a\u062d\u2026'); try{ const R=S.pm||(S.pm=await api('listPermits',S.tok)); D.permits=R.L||(R.L=R.rows.map(x=>obj(R.headers,x))); D.pstat=R.statuses||[]; }catch(e){ miss.push('\u0627\u0644\u062a\u0635\u0627\u0631\u064a\u062d'); } }
  if(tabOn('olap')){ say('\u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u062a\u062d\u0645\u064a\u0644 \u0623\u0648\u0644\u0627\u0628\u2026'); try{ D.olap=S.olap||(S.olap=await api('listOlap',S.tok)); }catch(e){ miss.push('\u0623\u0648\u0644\u0627\u0628'); } }
  D.miss=miss; return D;
}
async function xuBuild(opt,say){ say=say||(()=>{}); const D=await xuGather(say); say('\u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u0628\u0646\u0627\u0621 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0644\u0641\u2026'); await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,30)); const model=xuModel(D,opt||{charts:true}); const blob=await xuZip(xuParts(model)); return {blob,stats:model.stats,miss:D.miss}; }

   <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:10px"><button class="pri" id="xugo">\u062a\u0635\u062f\u064a\u0631</button><button onclick="closeModal()">\u0625\u063a\u0644\u0627\u0642</button></div>`);
  $('#xugo').onclick=async()=>{ $('#xugo').disabled=true; $('#xuerr').textContent=''; const say=t=>{ if($('#xust')) $('#xust').textContent=t; };
    try{ const r=await xuBuild({charts:$('#xuch').checked},say); const a=document.createElement('a'); a.href=URL.createObjectURL(r.blob); a.download='\u0645\u062a\u0627\u0628\u0639\u0629_\u0623\u0648\u0627\u0645\u0631_\u0627\u0644\u0639\u0645\u0644_'+String((S.B.project||{}).name||'').replace(/\s+/g,'_')+'_'+S.B.today+'.xlsx'; document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove(); setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href),60000);
      say('\u062a\u0645 \u0627\u0644\u062a\u0635\u062f\u064a\u0631: '+r.stats.sheets+' \u0623\u0648\u0631\u0627\u0642'+(r.stats.offices?'
