    catch(e){ if($('#xuerr')) $('#xuerr').textContent='\u062a\u0639\u0630\u0651\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u062a\u0635\u062f\u064a\u0631: '+e.message; }
    if($('#xugo')) $('#xugo').disabled=false; };
}
// ---------- Excel-like filters on the header of a plain table (forms, BOQ): F.cf = {column: [chosen values]} ----------
const cfStr=v=>String(v==null||v===''?'(\u0641\u0627\u0631\u063a)':v);
function thF(F,k,label,extra){ const on=F.cf&&F.cf[k]&&F.cf[k].length; return `<th${extra||''}><span style="white-space:nowrap">${label} <span class="thf${on?' on':''}" data-thf="${k}" title="\u0641\u0644\u062a\u0631">\u25be</span></span></th>`; }
function cfPass(F,x,get,skip){ const C=F.cf||{}; return Object.keys(C).every(k=>k===skip||!C[k]||!C[k].length||C[k].includes(cfStr(get(x,k)))); }
function cfWire(box,F,base,get,redraw){ box.querySelectorAll('[data-thf]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=e=>{ e.stopPropagation(); cfMenu(b,F,b.dataset.thf,base,get,redraw); }); }
function cfMenu(btn,F,k,base,get,redraw){ const old=$('#cfm'); if(old){ old.remove(); if(old.dataset.k===k) return; }
  F.cf=F.cf||{}; const cnt={}; base().filter(x=>cfPass(F,x,get,k)).forEach(x=>{ const v=cfStr(get(x,k)); cnt[v]=(cnt[v]||0)+1; });
  const vals=Object.keys(cnt).sort((a,b)=>{ const na=+a, nb=+b; return !isNaN(na)&&!isNaN(nb)&&a!==''&&b!==''?na-nb:a.localeCompare(b,'ar'); }); let chosen=new Set(F.cf[k]&&F.cf[k].length?F.cf[k]:vals);
  const m=document.createElement('div'); m.id='cfm'; m.dataset.k=k; m.className='cfm'; const r=btn.getBoundingClientRect();
  m.style.top=(r.bottom+window.scrollY+4)+'px'; m.style.left=Math.max(8,Math.min(r.left+window.scrollX-200,window.innerWidth-290))+'px';
  const list=q=>vals.filter(v=>!q||v.includes(q)).map(v=>`<label><input type="checkbox" data-cv="${esc(v)}" ${chosen.has(v)?'checked':''}> <span>${esc(v)}</span> <span class="muted">(${cnt[v]})</span></label>`).join('')||'<div class="muted">\u0644\u0627 \u0646\u062a\u0627\u0626\u062c</div>';
  m.innerHTML=`<input id="cfq" placeholder="\u0628\u062d\u062b \u0641\u064a \u0627\u0644\u0642\u064a\u0645"><div class="bar" style="margin:6px 0;gap:6px"><button id="cfa">\u0627\u0644\u0643\u0644</button><button id="cfn">\u0644\u0627 \u0634\u064a\u0621</button><span class="sp"></span><button id="cfc">\u0645\u0633\u062d \u0627\u0644\u0641\u0644\u062a\u0631</button></div><div id="cfl">${list('')}</div><div class="bar" style="margin-top:8px"><button class="pri" id="cfok">\u062a\u0637\u0628\u064a\u0642</button><button id="cfx">\u0625\u0644\u063a\u0627\u0621</button></div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(m);
  const wireL=()=>m.querySelectorAll('[data-cv]').forEach(c=>c.onchange=()=>{ if(c.checked) chosen.add(c.dataset.cv); else chosen.delete(c.dataset.cv); });
  wireL(); const q=m.querySelector('#cfq'); q.focus(); q.oninput=()=>{ m.querySelector('#cfl').innerHTML=list(q.value.trim()); wireL(); };
  m.querySelector('#cfa').onclick=()=>{ vals.filter(v=>!q.value||v.includes(q.value.trim())).forEach(v=>chosen.add(v)); m.querySelector('#cfl').innerHTML=list(q.value.trim()); wireL(); };
  m.querySelector('#cfn').onclick=()=>{ vals.filter(v=>!q.value||v.includes(q.value.trim())).forEach(v=>chosen.delete(v)); m.querySelector('#cfl').innerHTML=list(q.value.trim()); wireL(); };
  const done=v=>{ m.remove(); document.removeEventListener('mousedown',out); if(v!==undefined){ if(v&&v.length&&v.length<vals.length) F.cf[k]=v; else delete F.cf[k]; F.lim=0; redraw(); } };
  m.querySelector('#cfc').onclick=()=>done(null); m.querySelector('#cfx').onclick=()=>done(); m.querySelector('#cfok').onclick=()=>done([...chosen]);
  q.onkeydown=e=>{ if(e.key==='Enter'){ const m2=q.value.trim()?vals.filter(v=>v.includes(q.value.trim())):[...chosen]; if(!m2.length) return toast('\u0644\u0627 \u062a\u0648\u062c\u062f \u0642\u064a\u0645\u0629 \u0645\u0637\u0627\u0628\u0642\u0629'); done(m2); } if(e.key==='Escape') done(); };
  const out=e=>{ if(!m.contains(e.target)&&e.target!==btn) done(); }; setTimeout(()=>document.addEventListener('mousedown',out),0); }
function cfBar(F,labels,redraw,box){ const C=F.cf||{}, ks=Object.keys(C).filter(k=>C[k]&&C[k].length); if(!ks.length) return '';
  return `<div class="cfbar">${ks.map(k=>`<span class="pill">${esc(labels[k]||k)}: ${esc(C[k].slice(0,3).join('\u060c '))}${C[k].length>3?' +'+(C[k].length-3):''} <b data-cfx="${k}" style="cursor:pointer">\u2715</b></span>`).join('')} <button id="cfall">\u0645\u0633\u062d \u0643\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u0641\u0644\u0627\u062a\u0631</button></div>`; }
function cfBarWire(F,redraw){ document.querySelectorAll('[data-cfx]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{ delete F.cf[b.dataset.cfx]; redraw(); }); if($('#cfall')) $('#cfall').onclick=()=>{ F.cf={}; redraw(); }; }
// ---------- site drawing (\u0645\u062e\u0637\u0637 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0648\u0642\u0639): a grid in metres, symbols tied to contract items, quantities from the drawing ----------
// Shared by the program and the field app. Sketch.open(opts) shows a full-screen editor; opts:
//   data {v,n,s:[shapes]}, items {code:[desc,uom]}, meta {wo,office,district,eng,date,logo}, ro (read only),
//   onSave(data, pngBase64, qty) -> Promise, onQty(qty) (move quantities to the BOQ). Sketch.qty(data) -> [{c,q,u}]
(function () {
  const L = 'L', P = 'P';

    st: { k: L, n: '\u0634\u0627\u0631\u0639 (\u0645\u0631\u062c\u0639)', col: '#D9DDE2', w: 3, ref: 1 },

    earth: { k: P, n: '\u062a\u0623\u0631\u064a\u0636', fam: ['507', '309'], d: '507000001' },
    pole: { k: P, n: '\u0639\u0645\u0648\u062f', fam: ['20101'], d: '201010101' },
    rem: { k: P, n: '\u0625\u0632\u0627\u0644\u0629 \u0635\u0646\u062f\u0648\u0642', fam: ['504'], d: '504010001' },
    rep: { k: P, n: '\u0627\u0633\u062a\u0628\u062f\u0627\u0644 \u0639\u062f\u0627\u062f', fam: ['502'], d: '502020002' },
    mov: { k: P, n: '\u0625\u0632\u0627\u062d\u0629 \u0635\u0646\u062f\u0648\u0642/\u0644\u0648\u062d\u0629', fam: ['503', '3080103'], d: '503010001' },
    plot: { k: P, n: '\u0642\u0637\u0639\u0629 / \u0645\u0628\u0646\u0649', ref: 1 },
    txt: { k: P, n: '\u0646\u0635', ref: 1 }
  };
  const ORDER = ['dig', 'asph', 'tile', 'lv', 'mv', 'pipe', 'hdd', 'rr', 'ex', 'st', 'm1', 'm2', 'ct', 'cab', 'mp', 'lvb', 'sub', 'rmu', 'jt', 'term', 'earth', 'pole', 'rem', 'rep', 'mov', 'plot', 'txt'];
  const esc = s => String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, c => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' })[c]);
  const r2 = x => Math.round(x * 100) / 100;
  const segLen = p => { let t = 0; for (let i = 1; i < p.length; i++) t += Math.hypot(p[i][0] - p[i - 1][0], p[i][1] - p[i - 1][1]); return t; };
  // point symbols, drawn around (0,0), about 1.6 m wide
  function pt(t, lab) {
    const B = 'stroke="#14263F" stroke-width=".12"', box = (w, h, f) => `<rect x="${-w / 2}" y="${-h / 2}" width="${w}" height="${h}" rx=".15" fill="${f || '#fff'}" ${B}/>`;
    const tx = (s, c, z) => `<text x="0" y=".22" font-size="${z || .6}" font-weight="700" text-anchor="middle" fill="${c || '#14263F'}" font-family="Arial">${s}</text>`;
    switch (t) {
      case 'm1': return box(1.4, 1.4) + '<circle r=".3" fill="#14263F"/>';
      case 'm2': return box(1.7, 1.3) + '<circle cx="-.4" r=".28" fill="#14263F"/><circle cx=".4" r=".28" fill="#14263F"/>';
      case 'ct': return box(1.5, 1.4) + tx('CT');
      case 'cab': return box(1, 1.7) + '<line x1="0" y1="-.75" x2="0" y2=".75" stroke="#14263F" stroke-width=".08"/>';
      case 'mp': return `<rect x="-1.1" y="-.65" width="2.2" height="1.3" rx=".4" fill="#FFD400" ${B}/>` + tx('MP');
      case 'lvb': return box(2.2, 1.4, '#FFD400') + tx('LV');
      case 'sub': return '<rect x="-.9" y="-.9" width="1.8" height="1.8" fill="#fff" stroke="#B3261E" stroke-width=".12"/><line x1="-.9" y1=".9" x2=".9" y2="-.9" stroke="#B3261E" stroke-width=".12"/><text x="-.45" y="-.25" font-size=".55" font-weight="700" fill="#B3261E" font-family="Arial">T</text>';
      case 'rmu': return '<rect x="-1.1" y="-.7" width="2.2" height="1.4" fill="#fff" stroke="#B3261E" stroke-width=".12"/>' + tx('RMU', '#B3261E', .5);
      case 'jt': return '<circle r=".45" fill="#fff" stroke="#185FA5" stroke-width=".12"/><path d="M-.3 -.3L.3 .3M.3 -.3L-.3 .3" stroke="#185FA5" stroke-width=".12"/>';
      case 'term': return '<line x1="-.5" y1="-.6" x2="-.5" y2=".6" stroke="#185FA5" stroke-width=".18"/><g fill="#185FA5"><circle cx=".1" cy="-.45" r=".14"/><circle cx=".1" cy="-.15" r=".14"/><circle cx=".1" cy=".15" r=".14"/><circle cx=".1" cy=".45" r=".14"/></g>';
      case 'earth': return '<g stroke="#2E7D4F" stroke-width=".14" stroke-linecap="round"><line x1="0" y1="-.8" x2="0" y2="-.1"/><line x1="-.7" y1="-.1" x2=".7" y2="-.1"/><line x1="-.45" y1=".25" x2=".45" y2=".25"/><line x1="-.2" y1=".6" x2=".2" y2=".6"/></g>';
      case 'pole': return '<circle r=".6" fill="#3A3F47"/><circle r=".22" fill="#fff"/>';
      case 'rem': return box(1.4, 1.4) + '<circle r=".3" fill="#9AA3AE"/><path d="M-.9 -.9L.9 .9M.9 -.9L-.9 .9" stroke="#B3261E" stroke-width=".2"/>';
      case 'rep': return box(1.4, 1.4) + '<circle r=".3" fill="#14263F"/><path d="M.95 -.2A.75 .75 0 1 1 .45 -.85" fill="none" stroke="#E07A00" stroke-width=".14"/><path d="M.25 -1.1L.65 -.85L.25 -.6Z" fill="#E07A00"/>';
      case 'mov': return '<rect x="-.7" y="-.7" width="1.4" height="1.4" rx=".15" fill="#fff" stroke="#14263F" stroke-width=".1" stroke-dasharray=".2 .15"/><path d="M-1.9 0H-.85" stroke="#E07A00" stroke-width=".14"/><path d="M-.75 0L-1.1 -.25V.25Z" fill="#E07A00"/>';
      case 'plot': return '<rect x="-2" y="-1.4" width="4" height="2.8" fill="#EEF1F5" stroke="#5B6B7F" stroke-width=".08"/>' + `<text x="0" y=".2" font-size=".6" text-anchor="middle" fill="#5B6B7F">${esc(lab || '\u0642\u0637\u0639\u0629')}</text>`;
      case 'txt': return `<text x="0" y=".2" font-size=".7" text-anchor="middle" fill="#14263F" font-weight="700">${esc(lab || '\u0646\u0635')}</text>`;
    }
    return '';
  }
  // a line: its points as drawn by hand; the length is written by the engineer (s.len), nothing is measured
  const mid = p => { let t = segLen(p) / 2; for (let i = 1; i < p.length; i++) { const a = p[i - 1], b = p[i], d = Math.hypot(b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]); if (t <= d && d > 0) { const f = t / d; return [a[0] + (b[0] - a[0]) * f, a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * f, Math.atan2(b[1] - a[1], b[0] - a[0]) * 180 / Math.PI]; } t -= d; } const a = p[0]; return [a[0], a[1], 0]; };
  function lineSvg(s, sel) {
    const S = SYM[s.t] || SYM.ex, k = +s.k || 1, p = s.pts.map(q => q.join(',')).join(' '); if (s.pts.length < 2) return '';
    const W = S.w * k; let g = '';
    if (s.t === 'st') g += `<polyline points="${p}" fill="none" stroke="${S.col}" stroke-width="${W}" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-linecap="round"/><polyline points="${p}" fill="none" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="${.1 * k}" stroke-dasharray="${.8 * k} ${.6 * k}"/>`;
    else if (S.dbl) g += `<polyline points="${p}" fill="none" stroke="${S.col}" stroke-width="${.32 * k}" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-linecap="round"/><polyline points="${p}" fill="none" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="${.16 * k}" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-linecap="round"/>`;
    else g += `<polyline points="${p}" fill="none" stroke="${S.col}" stroke-width="${W}" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-linecap="round"${S.dash ? ` stroke-dasharray="${S.dash.split(' ').map(x => r2(x * k)).join(' ')}"` : ''}/>`;
    if (S.hatch) g += `<polyline points="${p}" fill="none" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="${.3 * k}" stroke-dasharray="${.08 * k} ${.35 * k}"/>`;
    if (S.tiles) g += `<polyline points="${p}" fill="none" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="${.25 * k}" stroke-dasharray="${.25 * k} ${.25 * k}"/>`;
    if (s.t === 'hdd') { const a = s.pts[0], b = s.pts[s.pts.length - 1]; g += `<circle cx="${a[0]}" cy="${a[1]}" r="${.3 * k}" fill="${S.col}"/><circle cx="${b[0]}" cy="${b[1]}" r="${.3 * k}" fill="${S.col}"/>`; }
    if (sel) g = `<polyline points="${p}" fill="none" stroke="#FFD400" stroke-width="${Math.max(W, .2) + .6}" stroke-opacity=".55" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-linecap="round"/>` + g;
    if (s.len || s.lab) { const [mx, my, ang] = mid(s.pts), up = ang > 90 || ang < -90 ? ang + 180 : ang, fz = .6 * Math.max(1, Math.sqrt(k)), fc = s.t === 'st' || s.t === 'ex' ? '#5B6B7F' : S.col;
      const T = (t, y, b) => `<text transform="translate(${r2(mx)} ${r2(my)}) rotate(${r2(up)})" y="${r2(y)}" font-size="${r2(fz)}"${b ? ' font-weight="700"' : ''} text-anchor="middle" direction="rtl" fill="${fc}" stroke="#fff" stroke-width=".12" paint-order="stroke">${esc(t)}</text>`;
      if (s.len) g += T('\u200F' + s.len + ' \u0645', -(.4 + W / 2), 1); if (s.lab) g += T(s.lab, .4 + W / 2 + fz * .8, 0); }
    return g;
  }
  function shapeSvg(s, i, sel) {
    const S = SYM[s.t]; if (!S) return '';
    if (S.k === L) return `<g data-i="${i}">${lineSvg(s, sel)}<polyline points="${s.pts.map(q => q.join(',')).join(' ')}" fill="none" stroke="transparent" stroke-width="${Math.max(1.4, S.w * (+s.k || 1) + 1)}" data-i="${i}"/></g>`;
    const k = +s.k || 1;
    return `<g data-i="${i}" transform="translate(${s.x} ${s.y}) scale(${k})">${sel ? '<circle r="1.5" fill="#FFD400" fill-opacity=".45"/>' : ''}${pt(s.t, s.lab)}${s.lab && s.t !== 'txt' && s.t !== 'plot' ? `<text y="1.35" font-size=".5" text-anchor="middle" fill="#14263F">${esc(s.lab)}</text>` : ''}<rect x="-1.3" y="-1.3" width="2.6" height="2.6" fill="transparent" data-i="${i}"/></g>`;
  }

  const CSS = `.sk-ov{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:9999;background:#fff;display:flex;flex-direction:column;font-family:inherit;direction:rtl;color:#1b1f27}
  .sk-top{display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;padding:8px 10px;background:#14263F;color:#fff}.sk-top b{font-size:15px;flex:1;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap}
  .sk-ov button{font:inherit;border:1px solid #cfd5dd;background:#fff;color:#1b1f27;border-radius:8px;padding:6px 10px;cursor:pointer}.sk-ov button.on{background:#FFD400;border-color:#14263F;font-weight:700}
  .sk-ov button.pri{background:#185FA5;color:#fff;border-color:#185FA5}.sk-top button{background:rgba(255,255,255,.14);color:#fff;border-color:rgba(255,255,255,.3)}.sk-top button.pri{background:#FFD400;color:#14263F;border-color:#FFD400;font-weight:700}
  .sk-tools{display:flex;gap:5px;overflow-x:auto;padding:6px 8px;border-bottom:1px solid #e3e6ea;background:#F6F7F9;flex:0 0 auto}.sk-tools button{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:2px;width:76px;flex:0 0 76px;font-size:10.5px;line-height:1.2;padding:4px 3px;white-space:normal;text-align:center}
  .sk-tools svg{width:34px;height:22px}.sk-sep{width:1px;background:#cfd5dd;margin:0 3px;flex:0 0 1px}
  .sk-main{flex:1;position:relative;overflow:hidden;touch-action:none;background:#fff}.sk-main svg{width:100%;height:100%;display:block;user-select:none}
  .sk-zoom{position:absolute;left:10px;bottom:10px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px}.sk-zoom button{width:40px;height:40px;font-size:20px;padding:0}
  .sk-north{position:absolute;right:12px;top:10px;width:54px;height:54px;border-radius:50%;background:#fff;border:1px solid #cfd5dd;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;cursor:pointer}
  .sk-hint{pointer-events:none;position:absolute;right:10px;bottom:10px;background:rgba(20,38,63,.88);color:#fff;border-radius:8px;padding:6px 10px;font-size:13px;max-width:70%}
  .sk-panel{border-top:1px solid #e3e6ea;padding:8px 10px;max-height:38vh;overflow:auto;background:#fff;font-size:13px}.sk-panel select,.sk-panel input{font:inherit;padding:5px;border:1px solid #cfd5dd;border-radius:6px;max-width:100%}
  .sk-row{display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:4px 0}.sk-q{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse}.sk-q td,.sk-q th{border-bottom:1px solid #eef0f3;padding:4px;text-align:right}.sk-q th{color:#5B6B7F;font-weight:600}
  .sk-q code{direction:ltr;unicode-bidi:embed;color:#714B67}`;
  function css() { if (!document.getElementById('sk-css')) { const st = document.createElement('style'); st.id = 'sk-css'; st.textContent = CSS; document.head.appendChild(st); } }
  function icon(t) { const S = SYM[t];
    if (S.k === L) return `<svg viewBox="-3 -1.2 6 2.4">${lineSvg({ t, pts: [[-2.6, .4], [-1, -.4], [1, .4], [2.6, -.3]] }, false)}</svg>`;
    return `<svg viewBox="-2.2 -1.4 4.4 2.8">${pt(t, t === 'plot' ? '25' : t === 'txt' ? '\u0646\u0635' : '')}</svg>`; }
  const bbox = sh => { let x0 = 1e9, y0 = 1e9, x1 = -1e9, y1 = -1e9; sh.forEach(s => { const k = (+s.k || 1) * 1.6; (s.pts || [[s.x, s.y]]).forEach(p => { x0 = Math.min(x0, p[0] - (s.pts ? 0 : k)); y0 = Math.min(y0, p[1] - (s.pts ? 0 : k)); x1 = Math.max(x1, p[0] + (s.pts ? 0 : k)); y1 = Math.max(y1, p[1] + (s.pts ? 0 : k)); }); }); return [x0, y0, x1, y1]; };
  // the drawing as a picture, with a header (order, place, engineer, date) and the names of the symbols used
  function toPng(data, meta) {
    const sh = data.s || []; let [x0, y0, x1, y1] = bbox(sh);
    if (x0 > x1) { x0 = 0; y0 = 0; x1 = 20; y1 = 14; }
    x0 -= 3; y0 -= 6; x1 += 7; y1 += 3; let w = x1 - x0, h = y1 - y0; if (w < 20) { x0 -= (20 - w) / 2; w = 20; } if (h < 12) { y0 -= (12 - h) / 2; h = 12; }
    const used = ORDER.filter(t => !SYM[t].ref && sh.some(s => s.t === t)), px = Math.max(8, Math.min(60, 1500 / w)), W = Math.round(w * px), Hm = Math.round(h * px), top = 110, rows = Math.ceil(used.length / 3), bottom = used.length ? 50 + rows * 34 : 20, H = top + Hm + bottom;
    const grid = []; for (let gx = Math.ceil(x0); gx <= x1; gx++) grid.push(`<line x1="${gx}" y1="${y0}" x2="${gx}" y2="${y1}" stroke="${gx % 5 ? '#F1F3F6' : '#E1E5EA'}" stroke-width="${gx % 5 ? .03 : .05}"/>`);
    for (let gy = Math.ceil(y0); gy <= y1; gy++) grid.push(`<line x1="${x0}" y1="${gy}" x2="${x1}" y2="${gy}" stroke="${gy % 5 ? '#F1F3F6' : '#E1E5EA'}" stroke-width="${gy % 5 ? .03 : .05}"/>`);
    const cw = (W - 40) / 3, leg = used.map((t, i) => { const c = i % 3, r = Math.floor(i / 3), x = W - 20 - c * cw, y = top + Hm + 46 + r * 34;
      return `<svg x="${x - 60}" y="${y - 20}" width="56" height="28" viewBox="${SYM[t].k === L ? '-3 -1.2 6 2.4' : '-2.2 -1.4 4.4 2.8'}">${SYM[t].k === L ? lineSvg({ t, pts: [[-2.6, 0], [2.6, 0]] }, false) : pt(t, '')}</svg><text x="${x - 68}" y="${y}" font-size="15" direction="rtl" text-anchor="start" fill="#1b1f27">${esc(SYM[t].n)}</text>`; }).join('');
    const svg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${W}" height="${H}" viewBox="0 0 ${W} ${H}"><rect width="${W}" height="${H}" fill="#fff"/>
      <rect width="${W}" height="${top - 10}" fill="#14263F"/>${meta.logo ? `<image href="${meta.logo}" x="16" y="12" width="76" height="76"/>` : ''}
      <text x="${W - 20}" y="42" font-size="26" font-weight="700" fill="#fff" direction="rtl" text-anchor="start">\u0645\u062e\u0637\u0637 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0648\u0642\u0639 \u2014 \u0623\u0645\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0639\u0645\u0644 ${esc(meta.wo)}</text>
      <text x="${W - 20}" y="78" font-size="16" fill="#FFD400" direction="rtl" text-anchor="start">${esc([meta.office, meta.district, meta.eng, meta.date].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 '))}</text>
      <svg x="0" y="${top}" width="${W}" height="${Hm}" viewBox="${x0} ${y0} ${w} ${h}" font-family="Arial">${grid.join('')}${sh.map((s, i) => shapeSvg(s, i, false)).join('')}
      <g transform="translate(${x1 - 2.5} ${y0 + 3}) rotate(${data.n || 0})"><path d="M0 -1.6L.7 1.2L0 .7L-.7 1.2Z" fill="#14263F"/><text y="-1.9" font-size=".8" font-weight="700" text-anchor="middle" fill="#14263F">N</text></g></svg>
      ${used.length ? `<line x1="20" y1="${top + Hm + 10}" x2="${W - 20}" y2="${top + Hm + 10}" stroke="#D9DEE5"/>${leg}` : ''}</svg>`;
    return new Promise((ok, no) => { const im = new Image(); im.onload = () => { const cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = W; cv.height = H; cv.getContext('2d').drawImage(im, 0, 0); ok(cv.toDataURL('image/png').split(',')[1]); };
      im.onerror = () => no(new Error('\u062a\u0639\u0630\u0651\u0631 \u062a\u062c\u0647\u064a\u0632 \u0635\u0648\u0631\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u062e\u0637\u0637')); im.src = 'data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,' + encodeURIComponent(svg); });
  }
  function open(o) {
    css(); const items = o.items || {}, meta = o.meta || {}, ro = !!o.ro;
    let D = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(o.data || { v: 1, n: 0, s: [] })); D.s = D.s || []; D.n = D.n || 0;
    const hist = []; let tool = 'sel', sel = -1, vb = null, dirty = false, drag = null, pins = {}, lp = null;
    const ov = document.createElement('div'); ov.className = 'sk-ov'; document.body.appendChild(ov);
    const main = () => ov.querySelector('.sk-main');
    const fit = () => { let [x0, y0, x1, y1] = bbox(D.s); const box = main().getBoundingClientRect(), ar = (box.width || 360) / (box.height || 400);
      if (x0 > x1) { x0 = 0; y0 = 0; x1 = 24; y1 = 24 / ar; } x0 -= 3; y0 -= 3; x1 += 3; y1 += 3; let w = x1 - x0, h = y1 - y0; if (w / h < ar) { const nw = h * ar; x0 -= (nw - w) / 2; w = nw; } else { const nh = w / ar; y0 -= (nh - h) / 2; h = nh; } vb = [x0, y0, w, h]; };
    const push = () => { hist.push(JSON.stringify(D)); if (hist.length > 60) hist.shift(); dirty = true; };
    const opts = (fam, cur) => { const L2 = Object.keys(items).filter(c => fam.some(f => c.indexOf(f) === 0)).sort(); if (cur && L2.indexOf(cur) < 0) L2.unshift(cur);
      return L2.map(c => `<option value="${c}" ${c === cur ? 'selected' : ''}>${c} \u2014 ${esc(((items[c] || [])[0] || '').replace(/^\s*\u0627\u0644\u0627\u0634\u0631\u0627\u0641 \u0639\u0644\u0649\s*/, '').slice(0, 70))}</option>`).join(''); };
    ov.innerHTML = `<div class="sk-top"><b>\u0645\u062e\u0637\u0637 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0648\u0642\u0639 \u2014 \u0623\u0645\u0631 ${esc(meta.wo || '')}</b>${ro ? '' : '<button id="sk-un" title="\u062a\u0631\u0627\u062c\u0639">\u21b6</button><button class="pri" id="sk-sv">\u062d\u0641\u0638</button>'}<button id="sk-x">\u0625\u063a\u0644\u0627\u0642</button></div>
      ${ro ? '' : `<div class="sk-tools"><button data-t="sel" class="on">\u270b<span>\u062a\u062d\u062f\u064a\u062f / \u062a\u062d\u0631\u064a\u0643</span></button><span class="sk-sep"></span>${ORDER.map(t => `<button data-t="${t}" title="${esc(SYM[t].n)}">${icon(t)}<span>${esc(SYM[t].n)}</span></button>`).join('')}</div>`}
      <div class="sk-main"><svg id="sk-svg"></svg><div class="sk-north" id="sk-n" title="\u0627\u062a\u062c\u0627\u0647 \u0627\u0644\u0634\u0645\u0627\u0644: \u0627\u0636\u063a\u0637 \u0644\u0644\u062a\u062f\u0648\u064a\u0631"></div><div class="sk-zoom"><button id="sk-zi">+</button><button id="sk-zo">\u2212</button><button id="sk-fit" style="font-size:13px">\u2922</button></div><div class="sk-hint" id="sk-h"></div></div>
      <div class="sk-panel" id="sk-p"></div>`;
    const $ = s => ov.querySelector(s), svg = $('#sk-svg');
    const hint = t => { $('#sk-h').style.display = t ? '' : 'none'; $('#sk-h').textContent = t || ''; };
    let live = null; // the stroke being drawn
    function draw() {
      if (!vb) fit(); svg.setAttribute('viewBox', vb.join(' '));
      const [x0, y0, w, h] = vb, g = [], step = w > 120 ? 5 : 1, sw = Math.max(1, w / 40);
      for (let x = Math.floor(x0 / step) * step; x <= x0 + w; x += step) g.push(`<line x1="${x}" y1="${y0}" x2="${x}" y2="${y0 + h}" stroke="${x % 5 ? '#EEF1F4' : '#D3D9E1'}" stroke-width="${(x % 5 ? .025 : .05) * sw}"/>`);
      for (let y = Math.floor(y0 / step) * step; y <= y0 + h; y += step) g.push(`<line x1="${x0}" y1="${y}" x2="${x0 + w}" y2="${y}" stroke="${y % 5 ? '#EEF1F4' : '#D3D9E1'}" stroke-width="${(y % 5 ? .025 : .05) * sw}"/>`);
      svg.innerHTML = g.join('') + D.s.map((s, i) => shapeSvg(s, i, i === sel)).join('') + (live && live.pts.length > 1 ? lineSvg(live, false) : '');
      $('#sk-n').innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="-2 -2 4 4" width="44" height="44" style="transform:rotate(${D.n}deg)"><path d="M0 -1.5L.6 1.1L0 .65L-.6 1.1Z" fill="#14263F"/><text y="-1.55" font-size=".7" font-weight="700" text-anchor="middle" fill="#B3261E" font-family="Arial">N</text></svg>`;
      panel();
    }
    function panel() {
      const p = $('#sk-p');
      if (sel >= 0 && D.s[sel]) { const s = D.s[sel], S = SYM[s.t];
        p.innerHTML = `<div class="sk-row"><b>${esc(S.n)}</b>${ro ? '' : `<span>\u0627\u0644\u062d\u062c\u0645</span><button id="sk-km" title="\u062a\u0635\u063a\u064a\u0631">\uff0d</button><b>${Math.round((+s.k || 1) * 100)}%</b><button id="sk-kp" title="\u062a\u0643\u0628\u064a\u0631">\uff0b</button><span style="flex:1"></span><button id="sk-del" style="color:#B3261E">\u062d\u0630\u0641</button>`}</div>
          ${S.k === L && !S.ref ? `<div class="sk-row"><span>\u0627\u0644\u0637\u0648\u0644 (\u0645)</span><input id="sk-len" type="number" step="0.1" min="0" inputmode="decimal" style="width:100px" value="${esc(s.len || '')}" placeholder="\u0627\u0643\u062a\u0628 \u0627\u0644\u0637\u0648\u0644" ${ro ? 'disabled' : ''}></div>` : ''}
          ${S.fam ? `<div class="sk-row"><span>\u0627\u0644\u0628\u0646\u062f</span><select id="sk-c" ${ro ? 'disabled' : ''}>${opts(S.fam, s.c || S.d)}</select></div>` : ''}
          ${S.area ? `<div class="sk-row"><span>\u0628\u0646\u062f \u0627\u0644\u0625\u0639\u0627\u062f\u0629</span><select id="sk-c2" ${ro ? 'disabled' : ''}>${opts(S.area.fam, s.c2 || S.area.d)}</select></div>` : ''}
          <div class="sk-row"><span>${s.t === 'txt' ? '\u0627\u0644\u0646\u0635' : s.t === 'plot' ? '\u0631\u0642\u0645 \u0627\u0644\u0642\u0637\u0639\u0629 / \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0628\u0646\u0649' : s.t === 'st' ? '\u0627\u0633\u0645 \u0627\u0644\u0634\u0627\u0631\u0639' : '\u0645\u0644\u0627\u062d\u0638\u0629 / \u0645\u0642\u0627\u0633'}</span><input id="sk-l" value="${esc(s.lab || '')}" style="flex:1;min-width:120px" ${ro ? 'disabled' : ''}></div>`;
        if (!ro) { $('#sk-del').onclick = () => { push(); D.s.splice(sel, 1); sel = -1; draw(); };
          const K = f => { push(); s.k = Math.round(Math.min(6, Math.max(.3, (+s.k || 1) * f)) * 100) / 100; draw(); };
          $('#sk-kp').onclick = () => K(1.25); $('#sk-km').onclick = () => K(.8);
          if ($('#sk-len')) $('#sk-len').onchange = e => { push(); const v = +String(e.target.value).replace(',', '.'); if (v > 0) s.len = r2(v); else delete s.len; draw(); };
          if ($('#sk-c')) $('#sk-c').onchange = e => { push(); s.c = e.target.value; draw(); };
          if ($('#sk-c2')) $('#sk-c2').onchange = e => { push(); s.c2 = e.target.value; draw(); };
          $('#sk-l').onchange = e => { push(); s.lab = e.target.value.slice(0, 60); draw(); }; }
        return; }
      p.innerHTML = `<div style="color:#5B6B7F">${ro ? '\u0639\u0631\u0636 \u0641\u0642\u0637.' : '\u0627\u0644\u062e\u0637\u0648\u0637: \u0627\u062e\u062a\u0631 \u0646\u0648\u0639 \u0627\u0644\u062e\u0637 \u062b\u0645 \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0645 \u0628\u0625\u0635\u0628\u0639\u0643 \u0645\u062a\u0635\u0644\u0627\u064b. \u0627\u0644\u0631\u0645\u0648\u0632: \u0627\u062e\u062a\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0631\u0645\u0632 \u062b\u0645 \u0627\u0636\u063a\u0637 \u0645\u0643\u0627\u0646\u0647. \u0627\u0636\u063a\u0637 \u0645\u0637\u0648\u0651\u0644\u0627\u064b \u0639\u0644\u0649 \u0623\u064a \u0639\u0646\u0635\u0631 \u062b\u0645 \u0627\u0633\u062d\u0628\u0647 \u0644\u062a\u062d\u0631\u064a\u0643\u0647\u060c \u0648\u0627\u0636\u063a\u0637 \u0639\u0644\u064a\u0647 \u0644\u062a\u0639\u062f\u064a\u0644 \u062d\u062c\u0645\u0647 \u0648\u0637\u0648\u0644\u0647 \u0648\u0628\u0646\u062f\u0647. \u0643\u0644 \u0645\u0631\u0628\u0639 = 1 \u0645\u062a\u0631 \u062a\u0642\u0631\u064a\u0628\u0627\u064b.'}</div>`;
    }
    const toM = e => { const r = svg.getBoundingClientRect(); return [vb[0] + (e.clientX - r.left) / r.width * vb[2], vb[1] + (e.clientY - r.top) / r.height * vb[3]]; };
    const snap = p => [Math.round(p[0] * 4) / 4, Math.round(p[1] * 4) / 4];
    const hitOf = e => { const t = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-i]'); return t ? +t.dataset.i : -1; };
    function setTool(t) { tool = t; sel = -1; ov.querySelectorAll('.sk-tools button').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('on', b.dataset.t === t));
      hint(t === 'sel' ? '\u0627\u0636\u063a\u0637 \u0639\u0644\u0649 \u0639\u0646\u0635\u0631 \u0644\u062a\u0639\u062f\u064a\u0644\u0647\u060c \u0623\u0648 \u0627\u0633\u062d\u0628 \u0627\u0644\u0634\u0628\u0643\u0629 \u0644\u062a\u062d\u0631\u064a\u0643\u0647\u0627' : SYM[t].k === L ? '\u0627\u0631\u0633\u0645 ' + SYM[t].n + ' \u0628\u0625\u0635\u0628\u0639\u0643 \u0645\u062a\u0635\u0644\u0627\u064b' : '\u0627\u0636\u063a\u0637 \u0645\u0643\u0627\u0646 ' + SYM[t].n + ' \u0639\u0644\u0649 \u0627\u0644\u0634\u0628\u0643\u0629'); draw(); }
    ov.querySelectorAll('.sk-tools button').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => setTool(b.dataset.t));
    const startMove = (i, m) => { sel = i; drag = { mv: 1, m, orig: JSON.stringify(D.s[i]), pre: JSON.stringify(D) }; live = null; try { navigator.vibrate && navigator.vibrate(30); } catch (e) {} hint('\u0627\u0633\u062d\u0628 \u0627\u0644\u0639\u0646\u0635\u0631 \u0625\u0644\u0649 \u0645\u0643\u0627\u0646\u0647'); draw(); };
    svg.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => { svg.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); pins[e.pointerId] = [e.clientX, e.clientY]; e.preventDefault();
      const ids = Object.keys(pins);
      if (ids.length === 2) { clearTimeout(lp); live = null; const a = pins[ids[0]], b = pins[ids[1]], d0 = Math.hypot(a[0] - b[0], a[1] - b[1]);
        drag = drag && drag.el != null ? { pinchEl: drag.el, d0, k0: +D.s[drag.el].k || 1, pre: JSON.stringify(D) } : (sel >= 0 && drag && drag.onSel ? { pinchEl: sel, d0, k0: +D.s[sel].k || 1, pre: JSON.stringify(D) } : { pinch: d0, vb: vb.slice() }); return; }
      const m = toM(e), hit = hitOf(e); clearTimeout(lp);
      if (!ro && hit >= 0) lp = setTimeout(() => { if (drag && !drag.moved) startMove(hit, drag.m0); }, 450);
      if (ro || tool === 'sel') { drag = { pan: 1, c: [e.clientX, e.clientY], vb: vb.slice(), m0: m, tapHit: hit, el: hit >= 0 ? hit : null, onSel: hit >= 0 && hit === sel }; return; }
      if (SYM[tool].k === L) { live = { t: tool, pts: [m] }; drag = { line: 1, m0: m, c: [e.clientX, e.clientY], el: hit >= 0 ? hit : null }; return; }
      drag = { tap: 1, m0: m, c: [e.clientX, e.clientY], el: hit >= 0 ? hit : null }; });
    svg.addEventListener('pointermove', e => { if (!pins[e.pointerId]) return; pins[e.pointerId] = [e.clientX, e.clientY]; if (!drag) return; const ids = Object.keys(pins);
      if (drag.pinchEl != null && ids.length === 2) { const a = pins[ids[0]], b = pins[ids[1]], d = Math.hypot(a[0] - b[0], a[1] - b[1]); if (!drag.moved) { hist.push(drag.pre); dirty = true; drag.moved = 1; }
        D.s[drag.pinchEl].k = Math.round(Math.min(6, Math.max(.3, drag.k0 * d / Math.max(drag.d0, 1))) * 100) / 100; sel = drag.pinchEl; draw(); return; }
      if (drag.pinch && ids.length === 2) { const a = pins[ids[0]], b = pins[ids[1]], d = Math.hypot(a[0] - b[0], a[1] - b[1]); const k = drag.pinch / Math.max(d, 1), cx = drag.vb[0] + drag.vb[2] / 2, cy = drag.vb[1] + drag.vb[3] / 2;
        const w = Math.min(400, Math.max(6, drag.vb[2] * k)), h = drag.vb[3] * w / drag.vb[2]; vb = [cx - w / 2, cy - h / 2, w, h]; draw(); return; }
      const far = drag.c && Math.hypot(e.clientX - drag.c[0], e.clientY - drag.c[1]) > 8;
      if (drag.mv) { const m = toM(e), dx = Math.round((m[0] - drag.m[0]) * 4) / 4, dy = Math.round((m[1] - drag.m[1]) * 4) / 4; if (!dx && !dy && !drag.moved) return;
        if (!drag.moved) { hist.push(drag.pre); dirty = true; drag.moved = 1; } const s = D.s[sel], o2 = JSON.parse(drag.orig);
        if (o2.pts) s.pts = o2.pts.map(p => [r2(p[0] + dx), r2(p[1] + dy)]); else { s.x = o2.x + dx; s.y = o2.y + dy; } draw(); return; }
      if (far) { clearTimeout(lp); drag.moved = 1; }
      if (drag.line && live) { const m = toM(e), a = live.pts[live.pts.length - 1]; if (Math.hypot(m[0] - a[0], m[1] - a[1]) >= Math.max(.15, vb[2] / 300)) { live.pts.push([r2(m[0]), r2(m[1])]); draw(); } return; }
      if (drag.pan && far) { const r = svg.getBoundingClientRect(); vb[0] = drag.vb[0] - (e.clientX - drag.c[0]) / r.width * vb[2]; vb[1] = drag.vb[1] - (e.clientY - drag.c[1]) / r.height * vb[3]; draw(); } });
    const up = e => { delete pins[e.pointerId]; if (Object.keys(pins).length) return; clearTimeout(lp); const was = drag; drag = null; if (!was) return;
      if (was.mv) { hint(''); if (!was.moved) draw(); return; }
      if (was.pinchEl != null || was.pinch) return;
      if (was.line) { const s = live; live = null; if (s && s.pts.length >= 2 && segLen(s.pts) >= .4) { push(); const pts = [s.pts[0]]; s.pts.forEach(p => { const a = pts[pts.length - 1]; if (Math.hypot(p[0] - a[0], p[1] - a[1]) >= .2) pts.push(p); }); const n = { t: s.t, pts }; if (SYM[s.t].lab) n.lab = SYM[s.t].lab; D.s.push(n); sel = D.s.length - 1; }
        else if (was.el != null && !was.moved) sel = was.el; draw(); return; }
      if (was.pan) { if (!was.moved) { sel = was.tapHit; draw(); } return; }
      if (was.tap && !was.moved) { if (was.el != null) { sel = was.el; draw(); return; } const p = snap(was.m0); push(); const s = { t: tool, x: p[0], y: p[1] };
        if (tool === 'txt' || tool === 'plot') { const v = window.prompt(tool === 'txt' ? '\u0627\u0644\u0646\u0635' : '\u0631\u0642\u0645 \u0627\u0644\u0642\u0637\u0639\u0629 / \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0628\u0646\u0649', ''); if (!v) { hist.pop(); return; } s.lab = v.slice(0, 60); }
        D.s.push(s); draw(); } };
    svg.addEventListener('pointerup', up); svg.addEventListener('pointercancel', up);
    svg.addEventListener('contextmenu', e => e.preventDefault());
    svg.addEventListener('wheel', e => { e.preventDefault(); const m = toM(e), k = e.deltaY > 0 ? 1.15 : 1 / 1.15, w = Math.min(400, Math.max(6, vb[2] * k)), f = w / vb[2]; vb = [m[0] - (m[0] - vb[0]) * f, m[1] - (m[1] - vb[1]) * f, w, vb[3] * f]; draw(); }, { passive: false });
    const zoom = k => { const cx = vb[0] + vb[2] / 2, cy = vb[1] + vb[3] / 2, w = Math.min(400, Math.max(6, vb[2] * k)), h = vb[3] * w / vb[2]; vb = [cx - w / 2, cy - h / 2, w, h]; draw(); };
    $('#sk-zi').onclick = () => zoom(1 / 1.4); $('#sk-zo').onclick = () => zoom(1.4); $('#sk-fit').onclick = () => { vb = null; draw(); };
    $('#sk-n').onclick = () => { if (ro) return; push(); D.n = (D.n + 45) % 360; draw(); };
    const close = () => { window.removeEventListener('resize', rs); ov.remove(); };
    const rs = () => { const v = vb; vb = null; fit(); if (v) { const c = [v[0] + v[2] / 2, v[1] + v[3] / 2]; vb = [c[0] - v[2] / 2, c[1] - v[2] * vb[3] / vb[2] / 2, v[2], v[2] * vb[3] / vb[2]]; } draw(); };
    window.addEventListener('resize', rs);
    $('#sk-x').onclick = () => { if (dirty && !ro && !window.confirm('\u0644\u0645 \u064a\u064f\u062d\u0641\u0638 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u062e\u0637\u0637. \u0625\u063a\u0644\u0627\u0642 \u0628\u062f\u0648\u0646 \u062d\u0641\u0638\u061f')) return; close(); };
    if (!ro) {
      $('#sk-un').onclick = () => { const h = hist.pop(); if (h) { D = JSON.parse(h); sel = -1; draw(); } };
      $('#sk-sv').onclick = async () => { const b = $('#sk-sv'); b.disabled = true; b.textContent = '\u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u0627\u0644\u062d\u0641\u0638\u2026';
        try { const png = D.s.length ? await toPng(D, meta) : ''; await o.onSave(D, png); dirty = false; close(); } catch (er) { b.disabled = false; b.textContent = '\u062d\u0641\u0638'; window.alert(er.message || er); } };
    }
    hint(ro ? '' : '\u0627\u062e\u062a\u0631 \u0631\u0645\u0632\u0627\u064b \u0623\u0648 \u0646\u0648\u0639 \u062e\u0637 \u0645\u0646 \u0627\u0644\u0634\u0631\u064a\u0637 \u0641\u064a \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0639\u0644\u0649'); draw(); setTimeout(() => { vb = null; draw(); }, 50);
    return { close };
  }
  window.Sketch = { open, SYM, toPng };

    if($('#bqap')) $('#bqap').onclick=async e=>{ if(dirty) return $('#bqerr').textContent='\u0627\u062d\u0641\u0638 \u0627\u0644\u062a\u0639\u062f\u064a\u0644\u0627\u062a \u0623\u0648\u0644\u0627\u064b'; e.target.disabled=true; try{ B=await api('boqApprove',S.tok,key,true); boqSync(key,B); draw(); toast('\u0627\u0639\u062a\u064f\u0645\u062f\u062a \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0642\u0627\u064a\u0633\u0629'); }catch(er){ e.target.disabled=false; $('#bqerr').textContent=er.message; } };
    if($('#bqun')) $('#bqun').onclick=async e=>{ e.target.disabled=true; try{ B=await api('boqApprove',S.tok,key,false); boqSync(key,B); draw(); }catch(er){ e.target.disabled=false; $('#bqerr').textContent=er.message; } };
    $('#bqdr').onclick=()=>{ const items={}; Object.values(I.m).forEach(x=>items[x[0]]=[x[1],x[2]]);
      Sketch.open({ data:B.draw, items, ro:!ed, meta:{wo:o.wo+(o.type?'-'+o.type:''),office:o.office,district:o.district,eng:o.site_engineer||S.B.user.name,date:S.B.today},
        onSave:async(d,png)=>{ const keep=dirty?L:null; B=await api('boqDraw',S.tok,key,d,png); L=keep||B.lines.map(l=>Object.assign({},l)); boqSync(key,B); toast('\u062d\u064f\u0641\u0638 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u062e\u0637\u0637'); draw(); } }); };
  };
  draw();
}
function boqSync(key,B){ const o=S.orders.find(x=>x.key===key); if(o){ o.boq_st=B.status; } if(S.bqList){ const r=S.bqList.rows.find(x=>x.key===key), n={key,lines:B.lines,status:B.status,draw:!!B.draw_id,upd_by:B.upd_by,upd_at:B.upd_at,appr_by:B.appr_by,appr_at:B.appr_at}; if(r) Object.assign(r,n); else S.bqList.rows.push(n); } }

        for(let r=hit.i+1;r<hit.A.length;r++){ const x=hit.A[r], c=norm(x[ci]); if(!/^\d{6,12}$/.test(c)) continue; rows.push([c,norm(x[di2>=0?di2:ci+2]),norm(x[ui]),x[pc],pp>=0?x[pp]:'',pa>=0?x[pa]:(pu>=0?x[pu]:'')]); }
        info='\u0645\u0646 \u0648\u0631\u0642\u0629 \u00ab'+hit.sn+'\u00bb: '+rows.length+' \u0628\u0646\u062f\u0627\u064b';
        $('#bqfp').textContent=info+' \u2014 \u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u0627\u0644\u062d\u0641\u0638\u2026'; const r=await api('boqImportItems',S.tok,rows); S.items=null; $('#bqfp').textContent=''; $('#bqfr').innerHTML=`<div class="card">\u2713 \u0627\u0633\u062a\u064f\u0648\u0631\u062f \u062c\u062f\u0648\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0633\u0639\u0627\u0631: <b>${r.n}</b> \u0628\u0646\u062f\u0627\u064b.</div>`; return; }
      // quantities
      const isKey=c=>/^\d{9}(-\d{3})?$/.test(c), mat=found.find(x=>x.h.some(c=>/^Item$/i.test(c))&&x.h.filter(isKey).length>=2);
      const cert=!mat&&found.find(x=>x.h.some(c=>/\u0627\u0644\u0631\u0645\u0632|^Item$/i.test(c))&&x.h.some(c=>/^(\u0631\u0642\u0645 )?[\u0627\u0623]\u0645\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0639\u0645\u0644$/.test(c)));
      if(cert){ const h=cert.h, ci=h.findIndex(c=>/\u0627\u0644\u0631\u0645\u0632|^Item$/i.test(c)), wi=h.findIndex(c=>/^(\u0631\u0642\u0645 )?[\u0627\u0623]\u0645\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0639\u0645\u0644$/.test(c)), pi=h.findIndex(c=>/\u062a\u0642\u062f\u064a\u0631/.test(c)), di=h.findIndex(c=>/\u0645\u0646\u0641\u0630/.test(c));
        for(let r=cert.i+1;r<cert.A.length;r++){ const x=cert.A[r], c=norm(x[ci]), wo=norm(x[wi]).replace(/\.0+$/,''); if(!/^\d{6,12}$/.test(c)||!wo) continue; rows.push({wo,c,p:pi>=0?x[pi]:'',d:di>=0?x[di]:''}); }
        info='\u0634\u0647\u0627\u062f\u0627\u062a/\u0645\u0642\u0627\u064a\u0633\u0629 \u0645\u0646 \u0648\u0631\u0642\u0629 \u00ab'+cert.sn+'\u00bb'; }
      else { if(!mat) throw new Error('\u0644\u0645 \u0623\u062c\u062f \u0623\u0639\u0645\u062f\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u0631\u0645\u0632 \u0648\u0631\u0642\u0645 \u0623\u0645\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0639\u0645\u0644');
        const h=mat.h, ci=h.findIndex(c=>/^Item$/i.test(c)), cols=h.map((c,i)=>isKey(c)?i:-1).filter(i=>i>=0);
        for(let r=mat.i+1;r<mat.A.length;r++){ const x=mat.A[r], c=norm(x[ci]); if(!/^\d{6,12}$/.test(c)) continue; cols.forEach(j=>{ const v=+String(x[j]).replace(',','.'); if(v) rows.push({wo:h[j],c,p:v,d:v}); }); }
        info='\u0643\u0645\u064a\u0627\u062a \u0645\u0646\u062c\u0632\u0629 \u0645\u0646 \u0648\u0631\u0642\u0629 \u00ab'+mat.sn+'\u00bb ('+cols.length+' \u0623\u0645\u0631\u0627\u064b)'; }
      rows.forEach(x=>{ x.p=x.p===''?'':String(x.p); x.d=x.d===''?'':String(x.d); });
      if(!rows.length) throw new Error('\u0644\u0627 \u062a\u0648\u062c\u062f \u0643\u0645\u064a\u0627\u062a \u0641\u064a \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0644\u0641');
      $('#bqfp').textContent=info+': '+rows.length+' \u0633\u0637\u0631\u0627\u064b \u2014 \u062c\u0627\u0631\u064d \u0627\u0644\u062d\u0641\u0638\u2026'; const r=await api('boqImportPlan',S.tok,rows); $('#bqfp').textContent='';
      $('#bqfr').innerHTML=`<div class="card">\u2713 ${esc(info)}: \u062d\u064f\u062f\u0651\u062b\u062a \u0645\u0642\u0627\u064a\u0633\u0629 <b>${r.orders}</b> \u0623\u0645\u0631\u0627\u064b.${r.skipped?' \u00b7 '+r.skipped+' \u0623\u0645\u0631\u0627\u064b \u0645\u0639\u062a\u0645\u062f\u0627\u064b \u0644\u0645 \u064a\u062a\u063a\u064a\u0631.':''}${r.missing.length?`<div class="err">\u0623\u0648\u0627\u0645\u0631 \u063a\u064a\u0631 \u0645\u0648\u062c\u0648\u062f\u0629 \u0641\u064a \u0627\u0644\u0625\u0633\u0646\u0627\u062f\u0627\u062a (${r.missing.length}): ${esc(r.missing.slice(0,30).join('\u060c '))}</div>`:''}${r.noItem.length?`<div class="err">\u0631\u0645\u0648\u0632 \u063a\u064a\u0631 \u0645\u0648\u062c\u0648\u062f\u0629 \u0641\u064a \u062c\u062f\u0648\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0633\u0639\u0627\u0631 (${r.noItem.length}): ${esc(r.noItem.slice(0,30).join('\u060c '))}</div>`:''}${r.dup.length?`<div class="muted">\u0623\u0631\u0642\u0627\u0645 \u0644\u0647\u0627 \u0623\u0643\u062b\u0631 \u0645\u0646 \u0646\u0648\u0639 \u0648\u0648\u064f\u0636\u0639\u062a \u0639\u0644\u0649 \u0627\u0644\u0646\u0648\u0639 \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0648\u0644 (${r.dup.length}): ${esc(r.dup.slice(0,20).join('\u060c '))}</div>`:''}</div>`;
      S.bqList=null; S.orders.length&&api('listOrders',S.tok).then(x=>{ const hi=x.headers.indexOf('boq_st'), wi=x.headers.indexOf('wo'), ci=x.headers.indexOf('ccode'); if(hi<0) return; const m={}; x.rows.forEach(r=>m[(r[ci]?r[wi]+'-'+r[ci]:r[wi])]=r[hi]); S.orders.forEach(o=>{ if(o.key in m) o.boq_st=m[o.key]; }); }).catch(()=>{});
    }catch(er){ $('#bqfp').textContent=''; $('#bqfe').textContent=er.message; } };
}

function loadXlsx(){ return window.XLSX?Promise.resolve():new Promise((ok,no)=>{ const s=document.createElement('script'); s.src='https:\/\/cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/xlsx@0.18.5/dist/xlsx.full.min.js'; s.onload=ok; s.onerror=()=>no(new Error('\u062a\u0639\u0630\u0651\u0631 \u062a\u062d\u0645\u064a\u0644 \u0642\u0627\u0631\u0626 Excel\u060c \u062a\u062d\u0642\u0642 \u0645\u0646 \u0627\u0644\u0625\u0646\u062a\u0631\u0646\u062a')); document.head.appendChild(s); }); }
start();
</script>
</body>
</html>
